13 jūṉ, 2024 aṉṟu vikkimūlattil iruntu pativiṟakkappaṭṭatu





cilampiṉ kati rā.cī





﻿ulakaḷāviya potuk kaḷa urimam (CC0 1.0)

itu caṭṭa ēṟpuṭiya urimattiṉ curukkam maṭṭumē. muḻu uriyi https://creativecommons.org/publicdomin/zero/1.0/legalcode eṉṟa mukavariyil kāṇalām.

patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēviyilli.

***

itu, ulakat tamiḻ vikkiyūṭakac camūkamum ( https://ta.wikisource.org ), tamiḻ iṇiyak kalvik kaḻakamum ( http://tamilvu.org ) iṇinta kūṭṭumuyaṟciyil, pativēṟṟiya nūlkaḷil oṉṟu. ikkūṭṭu muyaṟciyip paṟṟi, https://ta.wikisource.org/s/4kx eṉṟa mukavariyil virivākak kāṇalām.

nūl viḷakkam

mutaṟpatippu : jūli, 1998 nūl peyar : cilampiṉ kati āciriyar : rā. cīṉivācaṉ poruḷ : ilakkiyam patippakam : aṇiyakam 5, cellammāḷ teru, ceṉāy nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 030. tāḷ : mēp littō aḷavu : Crown 1 / 8 eḻuttu : 10 puḷḷi vili : rū.50/- oḷiyaccu : lēcar impreṣaṉ, ceṉṉi - 600 030. accakam : nelcaṉ piriṇṭiṅ ceṉṉi - 600 029.





﻿muṉṉuri

tamiḻil irāmāyaṇam pāratam ivaṟṟiṟku vantuḷḷa urinaṭi nūlkaḷ pōlac cilappatikārattukku ituvari yārum eḻuta muṉvaravilli. ataṉi iv urinaṭi nūl niṟivu ceykiṟatu.

itaṉ taṉic ciṟappu : mūla nūli oṭṭi ataṉōṭu ciṟitum piṟaḻamāl itu tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

maṟṟum “cilappatikārak kati” palarum aṟintatu. kāviyattip paṭittavar aṟivar eṉiṉum uḷḷē uḷḷa ellāc ceytikaḷiyum aṟivataṟku vāyppu illi. avvakiyil itu ellāc ceytikaḷiyum tarukiṟatu: muḻumiyāka aṟiya utavukiṟatu.

itu oru “nāṭṭuk kāviyam” - tamiḻ nāṭu, nāṭṭu makkaḷ avarkaḷ vāḻviyalit teḷḷatteḷiyat taruvatu itu. araciyal vāḻvil irunta cīrmi, peṇmiyiṉ uyarvu, aṟattiṉpāl nampikki immūṉṟum tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ uyarnta kōṭpāṭukaḷ. ivaṟṟic cilappatikāram uṇarttukiṟatu. ivvakiyil itaṉi oru “tirukkuṟaḷ” eṉṟē kūṟalām. tirukkuṟaḷiṉ virivākkamē cilappatikāram eṉalām.

tirukkuṟaḷ ceytikaḷi āciriyar nēril avar tam kūṟṟil kūṟukiṟār. itu katip pāttiraṅkaḷ vāyilāka uṇarttukiṟatu. tirukkuṟaḷip pōla ikkāviyamum tamiḻar aṟak kōṭpāṭukaḷi vaṟpuṟuttuvatu ākum.

urinaṭiyil paṭikkiṟapōtu moḻiccikkal ēṟpaṭuvatu illi; poruḷ viḷakkam nāṭat tēvi illi. iṉṟiya tamiḻ naṭi teḷivāṉa pōkkuṭiyatu; paṇpaṭṭa naṭi eḻuttāḷarkaḷ eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar. “vācakar naṭi” eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyam payilātvar eḻutum naṭi paḻaṅkālattu nili, iṉṟu tūya iṉiya eḷiya naṭiyil tara muṭikiṟatu. ituvum innūlukkuk kūṭutal ciṟappu eṉpatic cuṭṭik kāṭṭa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.

rā. cīṉivācaṉ

kaṇṇaki

mātavi

kōvalaṉ

tuṇip pāttiraṅkaḷ

aṇinalaṉkaḷ

iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ - oru matippīṭu





﻿patikam

ariciyal piḻittōrkku aṟamkūṟṟu āvatūum

uricāl pattiṉikku uyarntōr ēttalum

ūḻviṉi uruttuvantu ūṭṭum eṉpatūum

cūḻviṉic cilampu kāraṇamākac

cilappatikāram eṉṉum peyarāl

nāṭṭutum yāmor pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ

- iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ





﻿vāṉ ciṟappum vāḻttum





tiṅkaḷip pōṟṟutum tiṅkaḷip pōṟṟutum!

koṅku alartārc ceṉṉi kuḷirveṇ kuṭi pōṉṟu iv

amkaṇ ulaku aḷitta lāṉ

ñāyiṟu pōṟṟutum! ñāyiṟu pōṟṟutum!

kāviri nāṭaṉ tikiripōl poṉkōṭṭu

mēru valantirita lāṉ.



māmaḻi pōṟṟutum! māmaḻi pōṟṟutum!

nāmanīr vēli ulakiṟku avṉ aḷipōl

mēlniṉṟu tāṉcuratta lāṉ.

pūmpukār pōṟṟutum pūmpukār pōṟṟutum!

vīṅkunīr vēli ulakiṟku avaṉ kulattōṭu

ōṅkip parantoḻuka lāṉ.





﻿1. pukārk kāṇṭam

1. tirumaṇa vāḻttu

(maṅkala vāḻttup pāṭal)

pukār nakar

cōḻanāṭu kāviri pāyum vaḷamikka nāṭu; ataṉ talinakaram uṟanti eṉappaṭuvatu; ataṉ tuṟimukam pukār nakar ākum. itaṉip pūmpukār eṉṟaṉar. itu vaṇikac ciṟappuk koṇṭa vaḷamāṉa nakarāka viḷaṅkiyatu.

ic cōḻanāṭṭi āṇṭa maṉṉarkaḷ vīramum, koṭiyum, nīti vaḻuvā āṭciyum koṇṭavarākat tikaḻntaṉar. tiṅkaḷip pōṉṟu kuḷircciyum, ñāyiṟu pōṉṟu āṭciyum, maḻiyip pōṉṟu koṭic ciṟappum uṭiyavarākat tikaḻntaṉar.

intap pūmpukār imayattiyum, potikiyiyum pōl nilittu niṉṟatu; enta atircciyiyum kaṇṭatu illi; iṉpak kaḷippu mikka nakar atu; avvakiyil atu nākar nāṭṭiyum, tēvar ulakattiyum ottu viḷaṅkiyatu. makkaḷ entak kuṟiyumiṉṟi aṅku vāḻntaṉar. kuṭi peyarntu vāḻap piṟa iṭaṅkaḷit tēṭiyatu illi.



mānāykaṉ

ippūmpukār nakaril celvac ciṟappum, koṭic ciṟappum uṭiya vaṇikaṉ oruvaṉ vāḻntu vantāṉ. mānāykaṉ eṉpāṉ ac celvaṉ; vaṟiyavarkku īntu vāṉpukaḻ nāṭṭiṉāṉ.

avaṉ orē celvamakaḷ kaṇṇaki eṉpāḷ: avaḷukku vayatu paṉṉiraṇṭu ākiyatu. avaḷ kaṇkavarum pēraḻaku uṭiyavaḷ; ataṉāl avaḷi, “īkivāṉ koṭi yaṉṉāḷ” eṉṟu ciṟappittup pēciṉar. “vāṉattu miṉṉal” eṉa avaḷ pukaḻappaṭṭāḷ.

maṟṟum avaḷ pēraḻaku kāṇpavarik kavarntatu. tirumakaḷ vaṭivu ivaḷ vaṭivu eṉṟu pēciṉar; ivaḷ kaṟpiṉ tiṟam pārāṭṭappaṭṭatu. vaṭamīṉ ākiya aruntati aṉiyavaḷ eṉṟu av ūr makaḷir avaḷip pārāṭṭiṉar.

mācāttuvāṉ

atē ūril celvac ciṟappum, uyarmatippum peṟṟa vaṇikaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉ vāḻntu vantāṉ. avaṉ peyar mācāttuvāṉ eṉpatu ākum. avaṉ aracaṉum matikkattakka kuṭimakaṉ eṉṟu pārāṭṭappaṭṭāṉ. mānāykaṉip pōlavē vaṟiyavarkku vaḻaṅkuvatil mikka pukaḻ peṟṟiruntāṉ. avaṉukkum orē makaṉ kōvalaṉ eṉpāṉ; vayatu patiṉāṟu ākiyatu. avaṉ naṟkuṇatti aṉivarum pārāṭṭip pēciṉar. avaṉ aḻakil murukaṉ eṉṟu naṅkiyar matittup pārāṭṭiṉar. avaṉi “maṇtēytta pukaḻiṉāṉ” eṉṟu iḷaṅkō aṟimukam ceykiṉṟār. ulaku eṅkum avaṉ pukaḻ pēcappaṭṭatu.

maṇa nikaḻcci

iru kuṭiyiṉarum orē kulattic cārntavar; vaṇikap perumakkaḷ. ivarkaḷ tam makkaḷukku maṇam muṭikkak karutiṉar. kaṇṇakikkum kōvalaṉukkum tirumaṇam ceyvippatil ārvam koṇṭaṉar. nāḷ kuṟittuc ceytiyi nakarattukku aṟivittaṉar. avarkaḷ celva nilikkēṟpa mikavum āṭamparamākat tirumaṇatti naṭattiṉar.

ūravarkkuc ceyti aṟivippatilēyē avarkaḷ celvac ciṟappu veḷippaṭṭatu. yāṉiyiṉ piṭariyil aḻakiya makaḷiri amarvittu veṇ koṟṟak kuṭikaḷ puṭi cūḻac caṅkum muḻavum atira ūrvalamākac cella vittaṉar. anta ūrvalattil maṅkalattāliyum vikkappaṭṭatu. 'mānakarkku īntār maṇam” eṉṟu kūṟuvar kaviñar.

nāḷ pārttu naṭcattiram pārttu maṇam naṭattuvatu akkāla vaḻakkamāka iruntatu. purōkitari vittē tirumaṇam naṭattiṉar. cittiri mātam cantiraṉ urōkiṇiyi aṭiyum nāḷ naṉmukūrttamāka niccayikkappaṭṭatu. pantaliṭṭu maṇa mēṭiyi alaṅkarittaṉar. virat tūṇkaḷ pantalit tāṅkiṉa. nīlap paṭṭāṭi vitāṉamāka amintatu. pūkkaḷiyum, muttu mālikaḷiyum caraṅkaḷākat toṅkaviṭṭaṉar. pū iṭṭa pantalil muttukaḷ patikkap peṟṟu iruntaṉa.

mēṭiyil kaṇṇakiyōṭu kōvalaṉ amarntāṉ; muṉṉar vēḷvit tī eḻuppiṉar. maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷik kippiṭittut tīyi valam vantāṉ. avaḷ karattip piṭittuk koṇṭu valamāka vanta kāṭci aṅkiruntavar maṉatti makiḻvittatu. viyattaku kāṭciyākak koṇṭaṉar.

vāḻtturi

am maṇa maṇṭapattil maṅkiyarkaḷ kalakalappākac ceyalpaṭṭaṉar. pū aṇinta makaḷir polivu ūṭṭiṉar. iḷiya naṅkiyar aṅku vaḷiyac cūḻntu iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ mēṉiaḻaku kaṇṇik kavarntatu. ocinta nōkku avarkaḷ kacinta ārvattik kāṭṭiyatu. naṟumaṇam pūci avarkaḷ kavarcciyōṭu viḷaṅkiṉar. pāṭṭum uriyum avarkaḷ vāḻttukkaḷit terivittaṉa.

taṭṭukaḷil pūkkaḷiyum, cuṇṇattiyum, aṇi vakikaḷiyum tāṅkic ceṉṟaṉar. pūraṇa poṟkuṭam ēntiṉar. oḷiviḷakku ēntiṉar. vaṇṇam ēntiṉar. cuṇṇam ēntiṉar. pālikik kuṭam tāṅkiṉar. eṅkum maṅkalak kāṭciyit taṅka vittaṉar.

kūntalil pūkkaḷ cūṭiya maṅkala maṭantiyar āci kūṟiṉar. “iruvarum iṇi piriyāmal vāḻka” eṉṟu vāḻttuk kūṟi avarkaḷ mītu malarkaḷit tūvi nallamaḷiyil cērttaṉar.

"aracaṉ vāḻka! avaṉ āṭci ciṟakka!” eṉṟu vāḻttuk kūṟi am maṇaviḻā nikaḻviṉi muṭittaṉar.





2. illaṟa vāḻkki

(maṉiyaṟampaṭutta kāti)

kaṇṇakiyum kōvalaṉum

“aṉpum aṟaṉum uṭittāyiṉ ilvāḻkki

paṇpum payaṉum atu”





eṉpār vaḷḷuvar. avarkaḷ aṉpu vāḻkki iṉpattil toṭaṅkiyatu. kātalil kaḷittuk kavitipāṭi avaṉ ciṟanta kātalaṉ eṉpati veḷip paṭuttiṉāṉ. muṉpiṉ paḻakātavarkaḷ; kātalittu maṇam ceytu koṇṭavarkaḷ allar, maṇam ceytu koṇṭu kātalittavarkaḷ.

avarkaḷi iṉpa iravukaḷ iṇittaṉa. avaṉ “mutal iravu” eṉṟum maṟakka muṭiyāta nikaḻcci eṉṟutāṉ kūṟa muṭiyum. iṉpattiṉ elliyi mutaṟ cantippilēyē kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṇakiyi nēcittāṉ.

celvak kuṭumpattil piṟantavaṉ; ataṉāl eḻu nili māṭattil avaṉ mutal iravu toṭaṅkiyatu. iṭinili māṭattil iruvarum iruntaṉar; amarnta kāṭci atil iṉpavāḻvu toṭaṅkiṉar; neruṅkiṉar; kiḷarccikaḷ piṉpu avarkaḷit toṭarntaṉa.

teṉṟal kāṟṟuc cāḷaram vaḻiyāka uḷḷē nuḻintatu. atu palvaki malarkaḷiṉ vācatti aḷḷik koṇarntatu. vācamum kuḷircciyum mikka teṉṟal avarkaḷit tūṇṭiyatu; kātal uṇarvip perukkiyatu.

teṉṟaliṉ cukam avarkaḷi nilā veḷiccattit tēṭat tūṇṭiyatu. nilavu oḷiyil avarkaḷ kalavi iṉpattik kāṇa viḻivu koṇṭaṉar. nilāmuṟṟam ēḻāvatu māṭattil iruntatu. ataṉit tēṭi aṅkup pūkkaḷ parappiya paṭukkiyil avarkaḷ iṉpak kēḷikkikaḷit toṭuttaṉar.

avaḷ mārpilum, tōḷilum vaṇṇa ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭiṉāṉ. 'karumpum valliyum' kāma icciyit tūṇṭum cittiraṅkaḷ; avaṟṟic cempañcuk kuḻampil tīṭṭi avaḷ mēṉiyit toṭṭāṉ; avaḷ aḻakiṉik kaṇṭāṉ; iṉpak kaḷippiṉ ārampa akarāti atu.

cuṭṭum viḻikaḷ cūriya cantirarō eṉṟu pāṭiṉāṉ pārati; avaḷ vaṭṭak karu viḻikaḷ vāṉak karumukilō eṉṟāṉ. iṅkē avarkaḷ iruvarum cūriya cantirakaḷāka orē iṭattil amarntu puttoḷi ūṭṭiṉar. cūṭum kuḷircciyum avarkaḷiṭattil iṭam peṟṟaṉa. vilaki irukkumpōtu veppattiyum, arukil varumpōtu kuḷircciyiyum tantāḷ. avarkaḷ iruvarum taḻuvik koṇṭaṉar. itayam iṭam māṟiṉa eṉpar kampar. atu kātal; kalavi iṉpam atik kūṟumpōtu oruvar maṟṟavar āyiṉar eṉṟu kūṟuvatē ciṟappākum. "avaṉ tāṅkiya ceṅkaḻunīrt tārum avaḷ aṇintirunta mulli māliyum taḻuvik kalantaṉa" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl atu ciṟappāka amiyum.

tārum māliyum mayaṅka avaṉ taṉ vacam iḻantāṉ; taṉṉi iḻantāṉ; avaḷ pēraḻaki viyantu pārāṭṭiṉāṉ.

nalam pārāṭṭal

neṟṟiyiṉ aḻaku avaṉukkup piṟic cantiraṉi niṉivukkuk koṇṭu vantatu; "civaṉ taṉ caṭimuṭiyil tarittirunta piṟiyi avaḷukkut tirunutal ākuka eṉṟu koṭuttu viṭṭāṉ" eṉṟu pārāṭṭiṉāṉ.

avaḷ puruvaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷivu avaṉi vaḷittatu. kāmaṉvil atupōlak kaṇikaḷip poḻivatu avaḷ puruvaṅkaḷ; maṉmataṉiṉ vil eṉa muṭivu ceytāṉ. taṉ karumpu villiṉi avaḷukkup puruvamākap paṭittu anta maṉmataṉtāṉ tantuviṭṭāṉō" eṉṟu viyantāṉ.

avaḷ iṭi melintiruntatu; atu muṟiyāmal uṟutiyāka iruntatu: "atu intiraṉ tanta vaccirappaṭi" eṉṟāṉ.

avaḷ kaṇkaḷ vēlkaḷ eṉap pāyntaṉa. avaṉukku vētaṉi tantaṉa. avi avaṉip puṇpaṭuttiṉa. ataṉāl tākkap peṟṟāṉ. “avi kaṇkaḷ alla; vēlkaḷ” eṉṟāṉ. “murukaṉ taṉ vēliṉi iraṇṭāka uṭittu avaṟṟik kaṇkaḷ iraṇṭāka amittu viṭṭāṉ” eṉṟu pēciṉāṉ.

avaṉ kaṟpaṉikaḷ eṅkeṅkō celkiṉṟaṉa. avaḷ cāyal mayili niṉippūṭṭiyatu; naṭi aṉṉattik kāṭṭiyatu; iṉiya moḻi kiḷiyi aḻittatu.

“avaḷ cāyal kaṇṭu ataṟkut tōṟṟu mayil kāṭukaḷi aṭintuviṭṭatu; avaḷ naṭikku aṉṉam tōṟṟu naṉṉīrp poykiyi aṭintuviṭṭatu. amiḻtum yāḻum kuḻitta avaḷ iṉiyatoru kiḷavi kēṭṭuk kiḷi varuntukiṟatu. muḻumiyākak kaṟṟukkoḷḷa avaḷi viṭṭu nīṅkāmal uṭaṉ uṟikiṟatu”eṉṟu pārāṭṭiṉāṉ.

“tōḻiyar avarkaḷukkuk kōlam ceytavarkaḷ māperum tavaṟu ceytu viṭṭārkaḷ” eṉṟu pēcukiṟāṉ. “maṅkalattāli atu oṉṟu pōtum. piṅkala nikaṇṭupōl avarkaḷ piṟa aṇikaḷ aṇivittatu ēṉ?” eṉṟu kēṭkiṟāṉ; “mulit taṭattiṭi avarkaḷ alit tiṭal eṉa eḻutiya toyyil pōtumē” eṉṟāṉ. “muttu āram ēṉ aṇivittaṉar” eṉṟu kēṭkiṟāṉ. “naki atu miki” eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉ, “aḻakukku aḻaku ceyvatu vīṇ” eṉkiṟāṉ.

piṉ avaḷit toṭarntu mēṉi aḻakik kāṇkiṟāṉ; “poṉṉē” eṉkiṟāṉ. toṭṭup pārkkiṟāṉ; “valampuri muttu” eṉkiṟāṉ. nukarvil kaṇṭa iṉpam ataṉāl avaḷi “naṟu viri” eṉkiṟāṉ. itaḻc cuviyāl “karumpu” eṉkiṟāṉ. moḻic cuviyāl “tēṉ” eṉkiṟāṉ.

“mācaṟu poṉṉē! valampuri muttē! kācaṟu viriyē! karumpē tēṉē” eṉṟu aṭukkik koṇṭē pōkiṟāṉ. ulavāk kaṭṭuri palavaṟṟik kūṟukiṟāṉ.

kiṭittal arumipaṟṟi “aruntiṟal pāvāy” eṉkiṟāṉ. avaṉ vāḻvukku avaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātavaḷ eṉpatāl “āruyir maruntu” eṉkiṟāṉ. “aliyiṭip piṟavā amutu”, “maliyiṭip piṟavā maṇi”, “yāḻiṭip piṟavā ici” eṉṟu avaḷ arumikaḷik kūṟukiṟāṉ. avaḷ vaṇika makaḷ eṉpatil perumi koḷkiṟāṉ. 'kulamakaḷ' eṉpati aṟintu avaḷukku ataṉāl taṉi matipput tarukiṟāṉ. “peruṅkuṭi vāṇikaṉ perumaṭa makaḷē” eṉṟu aḻikkiṟāṉ. celvak kuṭiyil piṟanta ciṟappiṉic ceppuvatāka ak kūṟṟu amikiṟatu. avaḷ kūntalit taṭavik koṭukkiṟāṉ. “tāḻiruṅ kūntal tiyāl” eṉṟu viḷikkiṟāṉ. tāḻiruṅ kūntal virinta kūntalāka māṟum eṉpati avaṉ eppaṭi muṉ kūṭṭi uṇara muṭiyum!

aṟavāḻkki

tarukkumikka vāḻkki iṉpac cerukkōṭu tikaḻkiṟatu. iviṉpa vāḻkki avarkaḷ aṉpiṉi malarac ceyyat tuṇi ceykiṟatu. aṉpum aṟaṉum cērum pōtē vāḻkki payaṉmikkatāka māṟukiṟatu; malarkiṟatu.

kōvalaṉiṉ tāy ākiya avvīṭṭup pēriyal kiḻatti avaḷit taṉiyē kuṭi vittu urimiyuṭaṉ vāḻa vaḻi vakuttāḷ. aṉpōṭu aminta avvāḻvu aṟattōṭu iyintu paṇpum payaṉum koṇṭatāka muḻumi peṟṟatu. cuṟṟamum, aṟavōrkaḷum, viruntiṉarkaḷum avaḷ vīṭu tēṭi vantaṉar. cuṟṟam cūḻa iruntu ﻿ naṟṟavam mikka il vāḻkkiyi naṭattiṉāḷ. iv iṉpa vāḻkki cila āṇṭukaḷē nīṭittatu.

avarkaḷ aṭinta iṉpattukku etaṉi uvamiyākak kūṟuvatu? kāmattil kalantu makiḻum pāmpukaḷit tāṉ uvami kūṟa muṭiyum; ivarkaḷ niliyāmiyik kaṇṭu atu payaṉpaṭakkaḻiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷki uṭiyavar pōl vāḻkkiyi nēcittaṉar. iṭiyaṟāta makiḻvu kaṇṭaṉar.





3. mātaviyuṭaṉ toṭarpu

(araṅkēṟṟu kāti)

mātavi

āṇṭukaḷ cila kaḻintaṉa; ataṟkuḷ putiya toṭarpu eḻac cūḻalkaḷ uruvāyiṉa.

nāṭṭiya aḻaki mātavi āṭal, pāṭal, aḻaku immūṉṟilum tērntavaḷ āyiṉāḷ. avaḷ ūrvaci marapil vantavaḷ eṉṟu pēcappaṭṭatu. ataṟku oru katiyum kūṟappaṭṭatu.

intiraṉ aviyil cantiraṉ eṉat tikaḻnta cayantaṉik kātalitta naṭaṉamātu ākiya ūrvaci nāṭṭiyam piṟaḻntāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷ maṇṇulakil piṟakkac cāpam peṟṟāḷ. avaḷ cāpam iṅku ippukār nakaril itē nāṭṭiya araṅkil tīrntatu; akattiyaṉ aruḷāl cāpam nīṅkiyatu. eṉpar. attakiya ciṟappu mikka ūrvaci pukār nakaril piṟantāḷ. avvaḻi vantavaḷ mātavi eṉṟu ciṟappittup pēcappaṭṭatu.

kalic ciṟappu

aintil toṭaṅki ēḻāṇṭukaḷ avaḷ nāṭṭiyak kali payiṉṟu tērcci peṟṟāḷ. avaḷ kaliyi nāṭaṟiyac ceyya oru vāyppu; avaḷ nāṭṭiya araṅku ēṟi, aracaṉiṉ matippiyum pārāṭṭiyum peṟa virumpiṉāḷ. taṉ kalit tiṟaṉi naṉku maṉṉaṉukkuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭi araṅku ēṟiṉāḷ.

avaḷ āṭal kalikkut tuṇi niṉṟavarkaḷ nāṭṭiya ācāṉ; pāṭṭiyal pulavaṉ; icippulavaṉ; taṇṇumiyāḷaṉ (muḻavu iyakkupavaṉ); yāḻ vācippavaṉ kuḻalōṉ ittakiyavar āvār.

nāṭṭiya mēṭi

nāṭṭiya araṅku ciṟpa cāttira muṟippaṭi amikkappaṭṭa oṉṟu. ataṉ uyaram, nīḷam, akalam kuṟippiṭṭa aḷavukaḷik koṇṭu viḷaṅkiyatu. takkōṉ oruvaṉ kiviral aḷavu irupattu nāṉku koṇṭatu oru kōl eṉappaṭṭatu.

tariyil iruntu mēṭi oru kōl aḷaviṉatāka iruntatu; ataṉ akalam ēḻu kōl aḷavu eṉṟum, nīḷam eṭṭukkōl aḷavu eṉṟum, uyaram nāṉku kōl aḷavu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. tiric cīlikaḷ oru muka eḻiṉi, etirmuka eḻiṉi, karantuvaral eḻiṉi eṉṟu mūṉṟu vaki amikkappaṭṭaṉa. vaḻikaḷ iraṇṭu vikkappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. mēṭi mītu varuṇap pūtar cittiraṅkaḷ māṭṭi iruntaṉar. inta icik kaliñarkaḷ atteyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭē nikaḻccikaḷit toṭaṅkiṉar. ataṉ viḷakkukaḷ tūṇkaḷiṉ niḻalkaḷ taṭukkātavāṟu takka vakiyil amikkappaṭṭaṉa. talikkōl ataṉi mēṭiyil nilikkōlāka vittuc ciṟappuc ceytaṉar.

āṭal nikaḻcci

mātavi valak kāl eṭuttu vittu mitittu mēṭi mītu ēṟi valap pakkam cārntu niṉṟāḷ. tutipāṭum tōriya makaḷir iṭap pakkam ēṟi niṉṟaṉar. nikaḻccikaḷ toṭaṅkiṉa.

mutaṟkaṇ teyvap pāṭalkaḷ iraṇṭu at tōriya makaḷir pāṭiṉar. avaṟṟiṟkup pakka iciyākak karuvi iciyāḷar toṭarntu icittaṉar. avarkaḷ pāṭi muṭitta piṟaku mātavi āṭiyum, pāṭiyum taṉ kalit tiṟatti aviyōr aṟiyac ceytāḷ. nāṭaka nūl kāṭṭiya ilakkaṇam piḻiyātu palvaki āṭalkaḷiyum kūttu vakikaḷiyum āṭik kāṭṭiṉāḷ.

aracaṉ pārāṭṭu

aracaṉiṉ pārāṭṭi avaḷ peṟṟāḷ. ataṟku aṭiyāḷamākap paccili māliyip paricākat tantāṉ. ciṟapput tokiyāka āyirattu eṭṭukkaḻañcup poṉ tantu ciṟappittāṉ. talikkōl paṭṭattiyum avaḷukku aracaṉ tantu pārāṭṭiṉāṉ.

avaḷ tali araṅku ēṟiṉāḷ; aracaṉāl pārāṭṭap peṟṟāḷ; 'āṭaṟ celvi' eṉṟu aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭāḷ.

māli ēṟṟal

kaṇikiyar kulattil piṟantavaḷ āyiṉum oruvaṉōṭu vāḻa avaḷ virumpiṉāḷ. kaṇṇiyam mikka vāḻvil avaḷ karuttuc celuttiṉāḷ; takkavaṉit tēṭit tarac cittirāpati virumpiṉāḷ. paccili mālikku aracaṉ niccayitta antat tokiyik koṭuttu ammāliyi vāṅkuvārkku ivaḷ uriyavaḷ eṉṟu aṟivikkak kūṉi oruttiyi nakara nampiyar tiritaru maṉṟattukku aṉuppi vittāḷ.

kōvalaṉ ammāliyi āvaluṭaṉ vāṅkiyavaṉāyk kūṉi muṉ cella avaḷit toṭarntu mātaviyiṉ illatti aṭintāṉ. avaḷ aṇippilē akila ulakattiyum maṟantāṉ; viṭutal aṟiyā viruppiṉaṉ āyiṉāṉ. avaḷit toṭutalil kaṇṭa iṉpam avaṉi īrttup piṭittatu. vaṭu nīṅkiya ciṟappiṉi uṭiya taṉ maṉiviyiyum, vīṭṭiyum viṭutal ceytāṉ; maṟantāṉ.

mātavi ivvāṟu nāṭṭiyak kaliyi nilipeṟac ceytāḷ. avaḷ kali nilipeṟṟatu. araṅku ēṟi avaḷ pukaḻ mikkavaḷ āyiṉāḷ.





4. kaṇṇakiyiṉ pirivut tuyar

(anti mālic ciṟappuc cey kāti)

anti māli

mātavi makiḻntāḷ; kaṇṇaki tuyar uḻantāḷ. mālik kālam kūṭiyavarkku iṉpam aḷittatu; pirintu vāṭiyavarkkut tuṉpam tantatu.

inta mālippoḻutu nila maṭantikku vāṉtuyar tantatu. katiravaṉ maṟikiṟāṉ. kaṇavaṉik kāṇāmal varuntuvatu pōl immānila maṭanti varuntiyatu. ataṉ tici mukaṅkaḷ pacantu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. tiṅkaḷiṉ varukiyi atu āvalōṭu etirnōkkiyatu. kaṇavaṉi iḻantu kaṭuntuyar uḻanta nilamaṭanti taṉ āruyir makaṉ ākiya tiṅkaḷ eṅkē uḷḷāṉ eṉṟu etirnōkki iruntatu.

pēraracaṉ illāta nērattil kuṟumparkaḷ vantu nāṭṭu makkaḷi alikkaḻippatu pōl ñāyiṟu illāta nērattil inta mālippoḻutu vantu makkaḷi vāṭṭiyatu. nila maṭantiyi varuttiyatu.

makaḷir nili

inta mālik kālattil makaḷir nili yātu? kaṇavaṉi viṭṭup piriyātavarkaḷ kaḷimakiḻvu eytiṉar. pirintavar tuṉpuṟṟu varuntiṉar. mulli mukikaḷil vaṇṭukaḷ moyttu avaṟṟi malara vittaṉa; kār kālattil inta mulli malarcciyik kaṇṭu “pirintavar vantilar” eṉṟu makaḷir varuntiṉar. āyarkaḷ mullip paṇṇit tam kuḻaliciyil tōṟṟuvittaṉar; av ici avarkaḷi varuttiyatu.

makiḻcci poṅkum illattiṉar tattam vīṭukaḷil viḷakkēṟṟi vittaṉar. iṇintu iruntavar makiḻvu taruvatu māli eṉak karutiṉar mayakkattit taruvatu itu eṉṟu pirintavar karutiṉar.

mātavi makiḻcci

nilā oḷi vīciyatu; mālippoḻutu maṟintatu. mālip poḻutākiya kuṟumparkaḷit tiṅkaḷ ākiya pēraracaṉ vantu ōṭṭiyatu pōla nilavu veḷippāṭu iruntatu. inta nilavu oḷiyil kōvalaṉōṭu mātavi kulavi makiḻntāḷ. mañcattil avaṉ avaḷ koñcalil tiḷittāṉ. oppaṉimikku viḷaṅki avaṉukku ūṭalum, kūṭalum tantu uvakiyuṟac ceytāḷ.

ivaḷip pōlavē ēṉiya makaḷirum, uyir aṉiya tam koḻunaruṭaṉ avarkaḷ mārpil oṭuṅkik kaḷittuyil eytiṉar. anta iravu avarkaḷukku iṉpattit tantatu. avarkaḷ naṟumaṇam koṇṭu tammi aḻakupaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar. pūkkaḷ aṇintu polivuṭaṉ tikaḻntaṉar.

kaṇṇaki kaṭuntuyar

kaṇṇaki taṉimiyil vāṭiṉāḷ; avaḷukku vāḻkki kacantatu. aḻaku ūṭṭum aṇikaḷ avaḷukkuc cumiyāyiṉa. kālil aṇintirunta cilampu atik kaḻaṟṟi vittāḷ. kuṅkumam appik koṅkikaḷi aḻakupaṭuttuvati niṟutti viṭṭāḷ; tāli atu maṅkala aṇiyātaliṉ atu maṭṭum taṅka iṭam tantāḷ. ēṉiya aṇikaḷ avaḷi viṭṭu nīṅkiṉa. kātil aṇintirunta tōṭum atu kēṭuṟṟatu.

avaḷ neṟṟi avaṉ kūṭi iruntapōtu viyarttu makiḻvip pulappaṭuttiyatu. anta viyarvi avaḷi viṭṭup pirintatu.

añcaṉam kaṇkaḷukkuk karumi tantatu. ataṉit tīṭṭuvati viṭṭāḷ; tilakam iṭṭu neṟṟiyi muṉpu aḻakupaṭuttuvāḷ; atu avaḷ neṟṟiyi viṭṭu nīṅkiviṭṭatu. avaḷ cirippu kōvalaṉukku makiḻcciyiṉ virippu; ati avaṉ iḻantuviṭṭāṉ.

kūntal vāri muṭittu malar iṭṭu makiḻntilaḷ; atu neyyaṇitalum iḻantu vaṟaṇṭu ularntu kiṭantatu. kiyaṟu neñcōṭu avaḷ varuntic ceyal iḻantāḷ. aṭi mutal muṭi vari avaḷ oppaṉikaḷ veṟum coppaṉamāka maṟintaṉa. cōkattiṉ uruvamāka avaḷ cōrntu kiṭantāḷ.

ēṉiya makaḷir

anta iravuppoḻutu kaṇṇakiyi maṭṭum varuttavilli; peṇṇaṇaṅkukaḷ palariyum varuttiyatu. vēṉiṟpaḷḷi vētaṉi tarum eṉpatāl avarkaḷ kūtirp paḷḷiyit tēṭic ceṉṟaṉar. ataṉ cāḷaraṅkaḷil teṉṟal vīciyatu; pirintirunta avarkaḷi atu cuṭṭatu; vemmippaṭuttiyatu; puḻukkattit tantatu. ataṉāl kāṟṟum pukātapaṭi avaṟṟiṉ kaṇkaḷi aṭittaṉar. māli muttum, cantaṉak kottum avarkaḷukkuk kuḷircci taruvaṉa; avi avarkaḷukku vētaṉiyit tantaṉa. ataṉāl avaṟṟi aṇivatit tavirttaṉar. kuvaḷiyum, kaḻunīrp pūvum kuḷircciyi aḷippaṉa. avaṟṟit tūvip paṭukkiyil paṭuppatu vaḻakkam. pūviṉ aṭukku avarkaḷukku iṭukkaṇ tantatu. makiḻvi veṟuttaṉar. vemmimikka aṉṉat tūvi viritta virippil tam iravik kaḻikka virumpiṉar. aṅkum avarkaḷ kaṇkaḷ tuyil maṟuttaṉa. tuyiliṉṟi varuntiṉar vaṟaṇṭa vāḻkki avarkaḷukku veṟuppit tantatu. anta iravu avarkaḷukkup pakiyāka viḷaṅkiyatu. ūṭal kālattil ciṉantu civakkum avarkaḷ kaṇkaḷ inta vāṭal kālattil muttukkaḷ eṉa nīr ukuttuc cintiṉa; taṉimi avarkaḷi vāṭṭiyatu.

pukār nakar makaḷir

ippaṭi oru cilar varuntiṉar; eṉiṉum palarukku atu iṉpa iravākavē viḷaṅkiyatu. maṉmataṉiṉ paṭikaḷ makaḷir kūṭṭam eṉṟu kūṟuvatu marapu; inta makḷir tam ceyalāṟṟalāl āṭavarukku iṉpam aḷittaṉar. maṉmataṉ āṭci anta nakaril ōṅki viḷaṅkiyatu. yāmam kaḻintum avarkaḷ kāmam avintavarāy aṭaṅkik kiṭakkavilli. iravu nēram kalavikku uriya kaḷaṉāka amintatu; tam tuṇivarkkup pulaviyum kalaviyum tantu makiḻac ceytaṉar.

poḻutu viṭital

iravu kaḻintatu. poḻutu viṭintatu. aṉṉap paṟavikaḷ poykiyic cārntaṉa. āmpal kuvintatu; tāmari malarntatu; kuvaḷikaḷ kaṇviḻittaṉa; paṟavikaḷ muracam pōl ārttaṉa; kōḻikaḷ caṅku eṉak kūviṉa; vaṇṭukaḷ icittaṉa; ikkuralkaḷ aṉittum cērntu ūr makkaḷit tuyil eḻac ceytaṉa.

cōḻaṉ veṇ koṟṟakkuṭi nāṭṭiṉarkku niḻal ākiyatu. pakivarkku neruppu ākiyatu. atupōla inta iravu kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ iruntavarkaḷukku makiḻvu tantatu; pirintavarkaḷukku vētaṉi tantatu.





5. intira viḻāvum mātaviyum

(intira viḻavu ūr eṭutta kāti)

nakark kāṭci

pukār nakar kāliyil kaviṉ mikkatāka viḷaṅkiyatu; kāli oḷiyil ataṉ māṭaṅkaḷum, kōpuraṅkaḷum, kōyil talaṅkaḷum, maṟṟum uḷḷa maṉṟaṅkaḷum aḻaku peṟṟut tikaḻntaṉa.

inta nakar curucuruppāka iyaṅkiyatu; vaṇikarkaḷ mikku vāḻntaṉar; avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppup perumi tēṭit tantatu.

maruvūrp pākkam

maruvūrp pākkam eṉpatu nakariṉ uṭpakutiyākum. paṭṭiṉap pākkam eṉpatu kaṭaṟkariyi aṇukiya pakutiyākum. toḻilkaḷ mikka pakuti maruvūrppākkam; vāṇipam ceyvōrum, toḻil ceyvōrum vāḻnta pakuti atu; maṟṟiyatu uyar kuṭimakkaḷ vacitta iṭamākum.

māṭi vīṭukaḷum, aḻakumikka irukkikaḷum, māṉa kaṇ pōṉṟa caṉṉalkaḷ vitta māḷikikaḷum, poykik karikaḷil kavarccimikka yavaṉaratu vīṭukaḷum, vēṟṟu nāṭṭavar vacikkum nīr nilikaḷiṉ karikaḷil kaṭṭi irunta vīṭukaḷum annakari vaḷappaṭuttiṉa, aḻaku tantaṉa.

terukkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē iṉṉa iṉṉa toḻilukku eṉa vakippaṭutti iruntaṉar; naṟumaṇap poruḷ viṟpōr oru taṉitteruvil kuṭi iruntaṉar. nūl neyvōr taṉivītiyil iruntaṉar. paṭṭum, poṉṉum, aṇikalaṉkaḷum viṟpōr taṉivītiyil taṅki iruntaṉar. paṇṭaṅkaḷ kuvittu viṟṟavar teru kūlavīti eṉappaṭṭatu. appam viṟpōr, kaḷ viṟpōr, mīṉ vilipakarvōr, veṟṟili vācaṉip poruḷkaḷ viṟpōr, iricci, eṇṇey viṟpōr, poṉ veḷḷi cempup pāttirak kaṭikaḷ vittiruppōr, pommikaḷ viṟpōr, cittiravēlikkārar, taccar, kammāḷar, tōl toḻilāḷar, viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ ceyvōr, ici vallavarkaḷ, ciṟu toḻil ceypavarkaḷ ivarkaḷ ellām oru pakutiyil vāḻntu vantaṉār. intap pakuti maruvūrp pākkam eṉappaṭṭatu.

paṭṭiṉap pākkam

aṭuttatu paṭṭiṉappākkam; uyarnili māntar vacitta pakuti itu. aracaviti, tērviti, kaṭitteru, vaṇikar teru, antaṇar akkirakāram, uḻavar illam, maruttuvar, cōtiṭar, maṇikōttu viṟpavar, caṅku aṟuttu vaḷiyal ceyvōr ākiya ivarkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē vacittu vantaṉar.

kāvaṟkaṇikiyar, āṭaṟ kūttiyar, pūvili maṭantiyar, ēval peṇkaḷ, icikkaliñarkaḷ, kūttāṭikaḷ ivarkaḷ ellām orupakutiyil vāḻntaṉar.

aracaṉ araṇmaṉiyic cuṟṟip paṭi vīrarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppukaḷ iruntaṉa. yāṉi, kutiri, tēr, kālāḷ vīrarkaḷ iṅkuk kuṭi iruntaṉar. ippakuti kaṭaṟkariyi oṭṭi iruntamiyāl itu paṭṭiṉappākkam eṉap paṭṭatu. muṉṉatu ūr eṉappaṭṭatu; itu paṭṭiṉam eṉṟu pākupaṭuttik kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. iti vittuttāṉ pukār kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṟkup pūmpukār eṉṟum, pūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉṟum iruvēṟu peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.

palik koṭi

ivviru pakutikaḷukkum iṭippaṭṭa miyap pakutiyē ciṟappu mikkatāka viḷaṅkiyatu. kaṭikaḷ mikkuḷḷa pakuti, pakalil ceyalpaṭṭatu; itu nāḷaṅkāṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. iṅku eppoḻutum viṟpavar ōciyum, vāṅkuvōr ōciyum olittuk koṇṭē iruntaṉa.

maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭippakutiyē ivarkaḷ kaṭikaḷukkut tūṇkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉa; cōlikaḷ mikka pakuti atu eṉṟu terikiṟatu; iṅkē kāval pūtattiṟku oru pīṭiki amittiruntaṉar. atu palip pīṭiki eṉappaṭṭatu. intira viḻā koṇṭāṭum nāḷil iṅkup pūvum poṅkalum iṭṭu maṟakkuṭi makaḷir vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. vīrarkaḷ taṅkaḷ talikaḷi aṟuttu vittup pali iṭṭaṉar. “cōḻa aracaṉ veṟṟi peṟuka” eṉṟu vāḻttukaḷ kūṟiṉar.

intak kāval pūtam aṅku vikkappaṭṭataṟku oru kati kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. mucukuntaṉ eṉṉum cōḻa aracaṉ intiraṉukku utaviṉāṉ; acurarkaḷi ōṭṭiṉāṉ; avarkaḷ ivaṉukkut tīṅku karuti mantiraṅkaḷ colli ivaṉi aḻikka muyaṉṟaṉar. anta acurarkaḷiṭamiruntu kākka intiraṉ kāval pūtatti aṉuppivittāṉ. appūtam acurar ceyta vañcaṅkaḷi oḻittuk kaṭṭiyatu. aṅkēyē atu nilittu annakarukku atu kāval pūtamāka amintu viṭṭatu.

inta viḻā cittiri mātam cittiri muḻunilavu aṉṟu eṭukkappaṭṭatu. maruvūrppākkam, paṭṭiṉappākkam ivviru pakutikaḷiṉiṉṟum vīrarkaḷ iṅku vantu viḻā eṭuttup paliyum tantu koṇṭaṉar. itu viyattaku kāṭciyāka viḷaṅkiyatu.

cittira maṇṭapam

intiraviḻā kāṇa varuvōr ippalippīṭattik kaṇṭu viyappum makaḻvum aṭintaṉar. itaṉi aṭuttu annakaril pārkkat takunta kāṭciyāka viḷaṅkiyatu tirumāvaḷavaṉ amitta cittira maṇṭapam. avaṉ vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu veṟṟi koṇṭu tirumpi varukiyil pēraracarkaḷ avaṉukkut tiṟip poruḷāka mūṉṟu poruḷkaḷit tantaṉar. oṉṟu ‘koṟṟap pantar’; maṟṟoṉṟu ‘paṭṭimaṇṭapam’; maṟṟoṉṟu ‘tōraṇa vāyil’; vaccira nāṭṭu aracaṉ tantatu koṟṟappantar, makata nāṭṭu aracaṉ tantatu paṭṭimaṇṭapam; avanti vēntaṉ aḷittatu. tōraṇavāyil. ivi māṉuṭarāl ceyyappaṭṭavi alla; tēvataccaṉ mayaṉ ceytu tantavi; intiraṉ av aracarkaḷukku avvap poḻutu tantavi avi eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟi oruṅku vittu orē maṇṭapamākat tirumāvaḷavaṉ camittāṉ eṉpatu varalāṟu. itu cittira maṇṭapam eṉappaṭṭatu. intira viḻāvukku varupavar itaṉiyum kaṇṭumakiḻntaṉar.

aimperu maṉṟaṅkaḷ

maṟṟum annakaril kāṇat takuntavi aimperum maṉṟaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivi palvēṟu cōḻarkaḷ kālattil intiraṉāl aḷikkappaṭṭavi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

kaḷavu ceyvōri avarkaḷ taliyil kaḷavāṭiya potikaḷi aṭukkivittuc cuṟṟivarac ceyvatu ‘veḷḷiṭi maṉṟam’ eṉappaṭṭatu. nōy noṭiyāl vāṭuvōr kuḷattu nīril muḻuki eḻuntāl avarkaḷ nōy tīrntaṉar. antak kuḷam uṭiya maṉṟam ‘ilañcimaṉṟam’ eṉappaṭṭatu; pittam piṭittavar, nañcu uṇṭavar, aravu tīṇṭap peṟṟavar ivarkaḷ tuyar nīṅki uyarvu peṟṟaṉar. antap pakutikku ‘neṭuṅkalniṉṟa maṇṭapam’ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. tīya oḻukkattavar avarkaḷip puṭittu uṇṭu taṇṭittatu ‘pūta catukkam’ eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉ nīti tavaṟiṉālum, maṉṟaṅkaḷ tavaṟāṉa tīrppukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉālum kaṇṇīr ukuttu aḻutu kāṭṭi anītiyi eṭuttu urittatu; atu ‘pāvi maṉṟam’ eṉappaṭṭatu. ivi aimperu maṉṟaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iviyum viyattaku kāṭcikaḷ āyiṉa; ivviḻāvukku vantavar ivaṟṟik kaṇṭu viyantaṉar. viḻā aḻippu

vaccirak kōṭṭam eṉpatu intiraṉ kōyil; atil irunta muracatti eṭuttu yāṉiyiṉ piṭariyil ēṟṟi vippar. ataṉ mītu iruntu viḻāc ceytiyi ūrukku aṟivittaṉar.

kālkōḷ viḻā itu toṭakka viḻā, kālkōḷ viḻā mutal kaṭinili nikaḻcci vari naṭipeṟa uḷḷa nikaḻccikaḷ paṟṟi naviṉṟaṉar. maṅkala neṭuṅkoṭi eṭuttu ēṟṟiṉar.

nakarattu māḷiki muṉ eṅkum tōraṇaṅkaḷ toṅkaviṭṭaṉar. pūraṇa kuṭattil polinta muḷippālikiyi eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉar. pāvi viḷakkum, koṭic cīliyum, veṇcāmaramum, cuṇṇamum ēnti vitikaḷil polivu ūṭṭiṉar.

nīrāṭṭu viḻā

aimperum kuḻuviṉarum, eṇpērāyattiṉarum, araca kumararum, vaṇika iḷiñarum tattamakku uriya tērkaḷil ēṟic ceṉṟu, “aracaṉ veṟṟi koḷvāṉāka” eṉṟu, vāḻttuk kūṟi intiraṉukku nīrāṭṭu viḻā naṭattiṉar. kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ, kuṭaṅkaḷil kāvirinīr koṇṭuvantu intiraṉukku mañcaṉam āṭṭi nīr muḻukkuc ceyvittaṉar. inta nīrāṭṭutalē talimi viḻāvākak karutappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl itu nīrāṭṭu viḻā eṉavum ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.

ūrttiruviḻākkaḷ maṟṟum civaṉkōvil, murukaṉ, palarāmaṉ, tirumāl ikkōvilkaḷil avaravar marapuppaṭi pūcikaḷ naṭattiṉar. tēvarkkum, patiṉeṇ pūta kaṇaṅ kaḷukkum viḻā eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. aṟac cālikaḷil avarkaḷ viḻā eṭuttaṉar. ciṟippaṭṭa paki maṉṉari aracaṉ viṭuvittāṉ.

aṅkaṅkē ici nikaḻccikaḷ naṭi peṟṟaṉa. teṉṟal vīcum terukkaḷil kāmak kaṇikiyar kaṇkaḷukku viruntu aḷittaṉar, avarkaḷ vaṭivaḻakil mayaṅki āṭavarkaḷ avarkaḷōṭu uṟavu koṇṭaṉar.

intak kātaṟparattiyar pēraḻaku āṭavarkaḷit tikikka vittatu. vāṉattut tiṅkaḷ viyakattukku vantatō eṉṟu viyantaṉar. tirumakaḷit tēṭit tāmarimlar iṅku vantu pukuntatō eṉṟu pēciṉar. kūṟṟuvaṉ peṇ uruk koṇṭu emmip pētamip paṭuttukiṟāṉō eṉṟum añciṉar; vāṉattu miṉṉal maṇṇil vantu puraḷkiṟatō eṉṟu viyantu pēciṉar. ittakiya aḻaku uṭiyavarkaḷpāl tammip paṟikoṭuttuk kaḷi makiḻvu eytiṉar. avarkaḷōṭu tōyntatāl avarkaḷ mārpil eḻutiyirunta toyyil ivarkaḷ meyyil paṭiya atu ivarkaḷik kāṭṭik koṭuttatu.

ūṭal koḷvataṟku intak kāmak kaṇikiyartam uṟavu viṭṭu makaḷirkkuk kāraṇam ākiyatu. ūṭalit tīrppataṟku avarkaḷ paṭṭa pāṭu ēṭṭil eḻuta muṭiyātu; viruntiṉar vantāl ūṭal tīrvar. atuvum maruntāka amiyavilli; cuvimikka vāḻkki

mātaviyum kaṇṇakiyum

itu intira viḻāvil naṭipeṟṟa nikaḻccikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; inniliyil mātaviyiṉ nili yātu? ceṅkaḻu nīrppu tēṉcinti ukukiṟatu. atu pōl ivaḷ civanta kaṇkaḷ uvanta kāraṇattāl makiḻvak kaṇṇīr corintaṉa.

kaṇṇaki avaḷ karuṅkaṇkaḷ enta māṟṟamum aṭiyavilli; taṉimiyil uḻantu taḷarnta niliyil avaḷ kaṇṇīr ukuttāḷ.

mātaviyiṉ valakkaṇ tuṭittatu: kaṇṇakiyiṉ iṭakkaṇ tuṭittatu. iruvar nilikaḷ muraṇpāṭu koṇṭavi; orutti makiḻcciyil tiḷittāḷ: maṟṟorutti taṉimiyil tavittāḷ. inta māṟupaṭṭa nilikaḷil iruvarum makiḻvum tuyaramum kāṭṭiṉar.





6. kaṭaṟkari nikaḻccikaḷ

(kaṭalāṭu kāti)

vittiyātaraṉ viruppam

kōyil vaḻipāṭukaḷ orupuṟam kali araṅkukaḷ maṟṟorupuṟam. pukār nakaram iṉpa veḷḷattil nīntiyatu. inta viḻāvik kāṇa viṇṇavarum vantaṉar. viñciyarkaḷ kali nikaḻccikaḷik kāṇat tolitūram kaṭantu vantaṉar. avarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ vittiyātara vīraṉ.

avaṉ taṉ kātaliyōṭu, cōli oṉṟil kāma viḷiyāṭṭil makiḻvu kaṇṭāṉ. avaḷōṭu uriyāṭi intira viḻāvippaṟṟi viḷaṅkak kūṟiṉāṉ.

“cittiri mātam,cittiri nāḷ iṉṟu anta intira viḻā” eṉṟu eṭuttuk kūṟiṉāṉ. aṅkut tām kāṇa iyalum kāṭcikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟi avaḷi uṭaṉ aḻittu vantāṉ.

intiraṉ kaṭṭaḷiyāl pukār nakaril taṅkiviṭṭa kāval pūtattuk kōyil muṉpu āṇṭukkorumuṟi intira viḻā nāḷ aṉṟu vīrarkaḷ tammip pali iṭṭuk koḷḷum kāṭci viyappuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. “antap palip pīṭikiyik” kāṇalām. varuka” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.

“maṟṟum aivaki maṉṟaṅkaḷiṉ arumi perumi kaḷi nēril kāṇa iyalum” eṉṟāṉ.

“intiraṉ aviyil cāpam peṟṟup pukāril piṟanta ūrvaciyiṉ marapil vanta mātaviyiṉ āṭaliyum kāṇpōm” eṉṟāṉ.

vittiyātaraṉ varuki

iruvarum veḷḷimāl variyil iruntu puṟappaṭṭu imayattik kaṭantu vantaṉar. tēyaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi avaḷukkuk kāṭṭi aḻittu vantāṉ. ceḻumiyāṉa kaṅki pāyvati avaḷukkuk kāṭṭi makiḻvittāṉ. uñcimā nakarattiyum, viñcimā aṭaviyiyum, vēṅkaṭa maliyiyum, kāvirināṭṭiyum kāṭṭiya piṉṉarp pūmpukārkku aḻittu vantāṉ; aṅkaṅkē irunta kōyilkaḷit toḻutavaṉāki avaḷukkuk kāṭṭik kōyil viḻākkaḷil naṭipeṟum nikaḻccikaḷiyum uṇarttiṉāṉ.

ataṉpiṉ avaṉ peritum pukaḻntu pēciya mātaviyiṉ āṭal nikaḻcciyi avaḷukkuk kāṭṭat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. viṇṇavarum aṅkuvantu maṟintiruntu kaṇkoḷḷā ik kāṭciyik kaṇṭu kaḷimakiḻvu uṟṟaṉar.

mātaviyiṉ āṭal nikaḻccikaḷ

mātaviyiṉ āṭal araṅku toṭaṅkiyatu; mutal nikaḻcciyākat teyvap pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉar. tirumāliṉ pukaḻip pāṭiṉar. itu ‘māyōṉ pāṇi’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

ivvāṟē nālvaki varuṇappūtar pukaḻip pāṭiṉar. ataṉ piṉ tiṅkaḷip pōṟṟip pāṭiṉar. itu 'vāṉūr matiyampāṇi' eṉappaṭṭatu.

ataṉ piṉ kati potipāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉar. ivi teyvaṅkaḷ māṉiṭar naṉmikkāka ulakil āṟṟiya pōr nikaḻccikaḷic cittirittuk kāṭṭiṉa. teyvak katikaḷ itil iṭam peṟṟaṉa.

civaṉ tiripuram eritta uṭaṉ pārvatiyōṭu cuṭukāṭṭil āṭiya āṭṭam ‘koṭikoṭṭi’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

kalimakaṉ piramaṉ kāṇat tērmuṉ āṭiyatu ‘pāṇṭaraṅkam’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

kamcaṉit tuvamcam ceyta kaṇṇaṉ avaṉik kutti vīḻttiya nikaḻcci ‘alliyat tokuti’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

kaṇṇaṉ taṉṉi etirtta vāṇā cūraṉi etirttu avaṉik kaḷattil vīḻttiya ceyti ‘malliṉ āṭal’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

kaṭalil cūrapatumaṉi etirttu murukaṉ pōr toṭuttāṉ. atic cittirittuk kāṭṭiyatu ‘tuṭikkūttu’ eṉappaṭṭatu. avuṇarōṭu pōr ceyta murukaṉ avarkaḷi veṟṟi koṇṭu kuṭipiṭittu āṭiyatu ‘kuṭik kūttu’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

vāṇaṉ pērūrt terukkaḷil nīḷnilam aḷanta tirumāl āṭiya kūttu atu 'kuṭak kūttu' eṉappaṭṭatu.

āṇmi tirinta peṇmik kōlattil maṉmataṉ āṭiya kūttu ‘pēṭikkūttu’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

acurarkaḷ tām tāṅkiya pōrk kōlattit tavirttu oṭat turkki veṟṟi koṇṭa ceyti ‘marakkāl āṭal’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

atēpōla avuṇari etirttut tirumakaḷ veṟṟi koṇṭa ceytiyic ceppuvatu ‘pāvik kūttu’ eṉap paṭṭatu.

vāṇaṉ ūril vaṭakku vāyilil intirāṇi veṟṟi koṇṭa ceyti ‘kaṭiyam’ eṉappaṭṭatu.

ippatiṉoruvaki āṭalkaḷiyum mātavi avvav vēṭaṅkaḷil avvam marapukaḷ vaḻuvāmal naṭittu nāṭṭiyam āṭi nāṭṭavari makiḻvittāḷ naṭippu itu; vāḻkki avaḷi aḻittatu.

kali ulakil avaḷ taṉ toḻilit tiṟampaṭac ceytu inta ulakiṉari makiḻvittāḷ. kōvalaṉ ūṭaṟ kuṟippōṭu iruntāṉ; avaḷik kūṭi makiḻak kāttu iruntāṉ.

mātaviyiṉ oppaṉi

kātalaṉ avaṉi makiḻvikka avaḷtaṉ iyalpu nilikku vantāḷ. oppaṉi avaḷ uṭaṉ piṟantatu; avaṉ kalāracikaṉ; aḻaki ārātippavaṉ. kōlam ceytu avaṉi makiḻvikka virumpiṉāḷ. avaḷ taṉṉi oppaṉi ceytu koḷvatē taṉik kaṟpaṉiyāka iruntatu.

kūntali naṟumaṇam kalanta nīril kuḷippāṭṭiṉāḷ. naṟumpuki koṇṭu ularttiṉāḷ. kāl aṭimutal tali muṭivari avaḷ palavaki aṇikaḷip pūṇṭāḷ. iṅkē avaṉukku mātaviyākak kāṭci aḷittāḷ. kālviralukkuk kaṇiyāḻikaḷ; kaṇikkālukkuc cilampu vakikaḷ; toṭikkuc ceṟitiraḷ eṉṉum aṇi muttuppatitta mēkali avaḷ iṭikku aṇintāḷ. tōḷukkuk kaṇṭiki, vayiram patitta cūṭakam; maṟṟum palvaki vaḷiyalkaḷ avaḷ kikaḷil aṇintāḷ: kiviralukku makaramōtiram; kaḻuttukkup poṉ caṅkiliyum muttu āramum aṇintāḷ: cevikkuk kaṭippu: teyvutti, toyyakam mutaliyaṉa avaḷ talikku aṇintāḷ. ittaku oppaṉikaḷōṭu avaṉ makiḻa avaṉōṭu ūṭalum kūṭalum koṇṭu avaṉi makiḻvittāḷ.

putu viruppu

intiraviḻāvil kaliviḻākkaḷik kaṇṭa nakarattu māntar aṭuttatu kaṭalviḷiyāṭṭik kāṇak kaṭaṟkari nōkkic ceṉṟaṉar. mātaviyum aṅkuc ceṉṟu ataṉik kāṇa avāviṉāḷ. kōvalaṉum uṭaṉ puṟappaṭa icintāṉ.

vikaṟi viṭintatum kōvalaṉum mātaviyum kaṭaṟkari nōkkip puṟappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ payaṇam ullācap payaṇamāka amintatu. avaṉ attiri eṉṉum aḻakiya kutirimītu ēṟic ceṉṟāṉ. avaḷ viyam eṉṉum muṭuvaṇṭiyil amarntu ceṉṟāḷ.

vaḻik kāṭcikaḷ

celvam koḻitta annakariṉ peruṅkaṭikaḷ irunta pīṭikit teruvik kaṭantaṉar. atteruvil maṅkalattāciyar tattam vīṭukaḷil viḷakkukaḷ vittu aḻaku ceytaṉar. aṅkē celvam kuvintu kiṭantatu eṉpati avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷ viḷakkiṉa. malartūviya viḷakkukaḷ, maṟṟum māṇikka viḷakkukaḷ avarkaḷ illaṅkaḷi aḻaku ceytaṉa. avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷi alaṅkarittaṉar. aṇikalaṉkaḷ acintu oliceyya avvaḻakiyar atterukkaḷil tirintatu kāṭcikku iṉimi tantatu. celvac ciṟappāl atu tirumakaḷ irukki eṉa viḷaṅkiyatu. kaṭalvaḷam koṇṭu vantu kaṭikaḷ vittuk kuṭiyirunta anniya nāṭṭiṉar iruppukaḷiyum avarkaḷ kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉar.

aṅkē kūlamaṟukil paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṭikaḷ eṭuttu viṟṟaṉar. mālic cērikaḷ curucuruppāka iyaṅkiṉa. aṅkē cuṇṇamum, vaṇṇamum, cāntum, pākum, palakāra vakikaḷum viṟpōr viḷakkukaḷ oḷi viṭṭaṉa. ataṉi aṭuttu ovvoru toḻil ceyvōrum, kaṭikāṇi vittiruppavarum taṉiviḷakkukaḷ vittiruntaṉar. kaḷ viṟpavarum, mīṉ viṟpavarum viḷakkukaḷ vittu viṟpaṉi naṭattiṉar. iṭitta māvil veṇciṟukaṭuki vittatu pōlak kaṭaṟkari maṇalil ivviḷakkukaḷ oḷi viṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉa. tāmari malarkaḷ poykikaḷil pūttu viḷaṅkum maruta nilattip pōlat tāḻi malarkaḷ taḻittu viḷaṅkiya akkaṭaṟ karik kāṉalil avarkaḷ cērntu taṅkiṉar.

malipaṭu poruḷum, alipaṭu poruḷum maliyeṉak kuvintu kiṭakkum kaṭal tuṟimukam atuvum makkaḷ virumpit taṅkum iṭamāka iruntatu. at tuṟimukap pakutiyil kuḷir cōlikaḷil araciḷaṅ kumararum, avarkaḷ kātaliyarum taṅki makiḻntaṉar. avvāṟē vaṇika iḷiñarum, kaṇiki makaḷirum kalantu uriyāṭat taṉiyē tirikaḷ iṭṭut taṅki makiḻntaṉar. āṭukaḷa makaḷirum, pāṭukaḷa makaḷirum taṉittaṉiyē taṅki avarkaḷ kūṭu kaṭṭivāḻpavar pōl tirikaḷ taṭuttu otuṅki iruntaṉar; eḻiṉikaḷ avarkaḷukkut taṉimiyit tantaṉa.

kaḷimakiḻ kūṭṭam karikālaṉ kāviriyāṟṟil putup puṉal viḻā eṭutta nāḷil tiraṇṭiruntatupōl viḷaṅkik kāṭṭiyatu. nālvaki varuṇattup pālvakippaṭṭa makkaḷ āravārattuṭaṉ kāviri kalakkum caṅkamat tuṟiyil kuḻumi makiḻntaṉar.

amaḷiyil amartal

tāḻivirinta kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil vēli amittu otukkup puṟattil aṅkē oru puṉṉi mara niḻalil putumaṇal parappil oviyam varinta tiric cīli avarkaḷukku maṟipput tantatu. vitāṉam amitta veṇkāl kaṭṭilil mātaviyum kōvalaṉum amarntaṉar. arukirunta vacantamāli eṉṉum peyaruṭiya tōḻi niṉṟu koṇṭiruntāḷ. avaḷ kiyil irunta yāḻiṉik kōvalaṉ vāṅkiṉāṉ. avaṉōṭu ici pāṭum maṉaniliyil mātavi avaṉi etir nōkki iruntāḷ.





7. varip pāṭalkaḷ

(kāṉal vari)

yāḻ eṭuttu ici kūṭṭi avaṉ pāṭukiṟāṉ. mutalil avaṉ pāṭiyavi āṟṟuvarip pāṭalkaḷ; ivi nāṭṭu vāḻttāka amikiṉṟaṉa. aṭuttatu kāṉal varip pāṭalkaḷ. ivaṟṟil pukār nakarattu neytal nilattu makkaḷ vāḻvuc ciṟappuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu; maṟṟum ivi akap poruḷ tuṟiyamintavi ākiṉṟaṉa.

kāviri nōkkippāṭutal

“tiṅkaḷ kuṭi uṭiya cōḻaṉ ceṅkōl āṭci kaṅkivari paraviyuḷḷatu; avaṉ kaṅkiyi aṭintu avaḷōṭu uṟavu koṇṭālum nī veṟuppatilli; atu peṇkaḷiṉ kaṟpukku oru ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum” eṉṟu pāṭukiṟāṉ. itē karuttil aṭuttu “avaṉ kumariyi aṭintālum kāviri piṇakkuk koḷvatu illi” eṉṟu kumariyāṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭup pāṭukiṉṟāṉ.

“cōḻa nāṭṭil uḻavarkaḷ ōci irupuṟamum olikkak kāviri perumitamāka naṭakkiṟāḷ. ataṟkuk kāraṇam cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ vaḷamtāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟāṉ.

makaḷir āṇkaḷiṉ tavaṟukaḷip poṟuttuk koḷvatu avarkaḷ, kaṭami eṉṟa karuttum ivaṟṟil amintu viṭukiṟatu.

mātavi pāṭiyatu

ivaṟṟiṟku nikarāka mātavi pāṭum pāṭalkaḷ avaṟṟi maṟuttuk kūṟuvaṉapōl amikiṉṟaṉa.

“kāviriyiṉ perumita naṭikkuc cōḻaṉ ceṅ kōṉmitāṉ kāraṇam” eṉkiṟatu avaḷ pāṭal.

aṭuttu, “avaṉ veṟṟic ciṟappē ataṉ perumikkuk kāraṇam” eṉkiṟāḷ. “kāviri nīr uḻavarkku utavukiṟatu. atu tāyāka iruntu pāl ūṭṭuvatu pōla makkaḷukku nalvāḻvu tarukiṟatu. itaṟkuk kāraṇam cōḻaṉ makkaḷpāl koṇṭuḷḷa nēyamum, avaṉ kāṭṭum aruḷumtāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.

iṅkē muraṇpaṭṭa karuttukaḷ amintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa; peṇkaḷ perumikku āṇkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷē tuṇi ceyvaṉa eṉpatu avaḷ vaṟpuṟuttum karuttāka amikiṟatu.

avaṉ ceṅkōṉmi, veṟṟic ciṟappukaḷ, aruḷ taṉmi ivi kāraṇam eṉkiṟāḷ. eṉavē āṇkaḷ cemmiyākavum, makaḷirpāl nēyam mikkavarākavum naṭantu koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpati aṟivikkiṟāḷ. ivi kuṟippāka uṇarttappaṭum karuttukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa.

āṇkaḷ tavaṟu ceytālum peṇkaḷ poṟuppatu tāṉ takkatu eṉpatu kōvalaṉiṉ vātam ākiṟatu.

kāṉalvarip pāṭalkaḷ (kōvalaṉ)

āṟṟu variyit toṭarntu avaṉ kāṉal varip pāṭalkaḷ pāṭiṉāṉ; avaṟṟilum ittakiya muraṇ pāṭukaḷ amintuviṭukiṉṟaṉa.

talivaṉ kaḷavu oḻukkattil avaḷic cantittut taṉ kātalit terivikkiṟāṉ. acceytikaḷit tōḻi vivarikkiṉṟāḷ.

“taliviyiṉ muṉ kaṭal teyvattic cāṉṟākak kāṭṭit talivaṉ taṉ uṟutiyi urittāṉ. avaṉ piṉ avaḷit toṭarntu maṇam ceytu koḷḷa villi” atic cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟāḷ tōḻi. “kātalarākik kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil kiyuṟi tantu empiṉṉāl vantavar iṉṟu yārō pōl naṭantu koḷkiṟār” eṉpatum tōḻiyiṉ aṟivippu.

“nāṅkaḷ veḷuttatu ellām pāl eṉṟu nampi viṭupavarkaḷ maṟṟum maṟuttuk kūṟum āṟṟalum eṅkaḷukku illi” eṉkiṟāḷ tōḻi.

immūṉṟum talivaṉ taliviyariṉ kātal aṟimukac ceytikaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa; ivi tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟil aṟivikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; piṉpu naṭantatu yātu?

taliviyiṉ perumiyit talivaṉ viritturippaṉa piṉ varum pāṭalkaḷ. ivi talivaṉ kūṟṟukaḷ:

“kaṇ ceyta nōy avaḷ aṇipputāṉ tīrkkum; nōykku avaḷ kāraṇam; atu tīrkkum maruntu avaḷ muyakkē ākum”

“kaṭaṟkarik kāṉalil avaḷip peṇ eṉak koṇṭēṉ; illi; avaḷ varuttum teyvam eṉpati ippoḻutu aṟikiṉṟēṉ”

“avaḷ peṇ alla; uyir uṇṇum kūṟṟuvaṉ ākiṉṟāḷ”

“avaḷ vaṭivik kūṟuvatu eṉṟāl avaḷ mukam tiṅkaḷ; atu vāṉattu aravu añci iṅku oḷittatu; atu teriyāmal peṇ eṉṟu mayaṅkiviṭṭēṉ”

“avaḷ kaṇkaḷ avi koṭumi mikkavi, aruḷ ceyyavilli; ataṉāl avi kaṭuṅkūṟṟam ākiṉṟaṉa””

“kūntal aḻaku uṭiya avaḷ peṇ allaḷ; kaṇṭavari varuttum aṇaṅku ākiya teyvam āvāḷ” “eṉṉi varuttiyavi avaḷ pēraḻaku avaḷ moḻi; vaṉamuli; mukam; puruvam; miṉṉal iṭi; ivi ellām cērntu eṉṉi varuttiviṭṭaṉa”

“avaḷik kaṭaṟkariyil kaṇṭēṉ; antac cūḻnili eṉṉi varuttiyatu; avaḷ virinta kuḻal; mati pōṉṟa mukam, kayalpōṉṟa viḻikaḷ ellām cērntu eṉṉi varuttukiṉṟaṉa.”

“avaḷ iḷanaki, matimukam, iḷiyavaḷ iṇi mulikaḷ eṉṉi iṭar ceytuviṭṭaṉa”

“kaṭal pukuntu uyir vavvuvār avaḷ talivarkaḷ; avaḷ eṉ uṭal pukuntu uyir vavvukiṉṟāḷ; avaḷ virumpattakka mulikaḷ avaḷukkuc cumi; iṭi atu cumakkiṟatu; iṭar uṟukiṟatu”

“valiyāl uyir koḷvar avar talivarkaḷ; avaḷ kaṇṇākiya valiyil eṉ uyirik koḷkiṟāḷ; miṉṉal pōṉṟatu avaḷ iṭi, atu muli cumakkātu; iṟṟu viṭum”

“ōṭum paṭaku koṇṭu avaḷ talivarkaḷ uyirkaḷic cāṭuvar. avaḷ iṭi uḻantu varuntukiṟatu; cumi tāṅkātu”

“pavaḷa ulakkiyik kiyāl paṟṟi veṇ muttam kuṟuvāḷ; avaḷ kaṇkaḷ kuvaḷiyalla; koṭiyavi”

“puṉṉi niḻal pulavut tirivāy aṉṉam naṭakka avaḷ naṭakkiṉṟāḷ; avaḷ kaṇkaḷ kūṟṟam ākum”

“nīla malarik kiyil ēntip paṟavikaḷik kaṭivāḷ; avaḷ kaṇkaḷ vēliṉum koṭiya vāṭṭum taṉmiya” “aṉṉamē nī avaḷ arukil cellātē! avaḷ naṭikku nī ovvāy, ataṉāl nī ceṉṟu uṟavu koḷḷātē”

avaḷ koṭumiyi eṭuttuk kūṟukiṟāṉ talivaṉ; icceytiyik kōvalaṉ potuppaṭiyākavē vittu ippāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉāṉ.

mātavi pāṭiyatu

avaṉ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷil uḷkaruttu uḷḷatu eṉṟu mātavi tavaṟākak koḷkiṟāḷ. kalaviyil makiḻntavaḷ pōl kāṭṭik koḷḷukiṟāḷ. ataṉit toṭarntu avaṉpāl pulavi koṇṭavaḷ pōla yāḻik kiyil vāṅki avaḷum pāṭat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷ appāṭalkaḷukku viṭi taruvaṉa pōl amittut tarukiṟāḷ.

avaḷ pāṭalik kēṭṭu nilatteyvam viyappu eytiyatu; nīḷ nilattōr makiḻvu eytiṉar; yāḻiciyōṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalanta taṉ iṉiya kuralil avaḷ pāṭiṉāḷ.

avaṉ pāṭiya āṟṟu varip pāṭalkaḷukku ivaḷ viṭi tantāḷ. “āṇkaḷ cīrōṭu iruntāltāṉ peṇkaḷukku matippu uṇṭu” eṉṟu terivittāḷ.

atē pōlak kāṉal varippāṭalkaḷiyum ivaḷum toṭarntu pāṭukiṟāḷ; avaṉ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ aṉittum peṇ koṭiyavaḷ eṉṟa karutti vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟuvaṉa pōla amintaṉa. ataṟku ivaḷ maṟuppuriyāka “āṇkaḷ tām irakkamaṟṟavarkaḷ; peṇkaḷiṉ nuṇṇuṇarviyum, tuṉpattiyum aṟiyāta varkaḷ” eṉṟu cāṭukiṟāḷ. appāṭalkaḷ karuttukaḷ piṉvarumāṟu;

"muttukaḷ tantu eṅkaḷit talivaṉ mayakka virumpukiṟāṉ; avi avaḷ pallukku nikarākā, kaṭal alikaḷ kari cērkkum muttukaḷē eṅkaḷukkup pōtumāṉavi; avaṉ kiyuṟi tēvi illi” eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟi maṟuttuviṭukiṉṟāḷ. itu tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟāka amikiṟatu.

“kaḷavil vantu kalantāṉ; avaṉ pirintatāl talivi melintāḷ. avaḷ kivaḷiyalkaḷ kaḻaṉṟu viḻuntu viṭukiṉṟaṉa”.

“kaḷ uṇṭāl mayakkam varum eṉṟu eṅkaḷukkut teriyum; kāmam koṇṭāl atuvum nōy tarum eṉpati ippoḻututāṉ aṟikiṟōm” eṉkiṟāḷ tōḻi. ivi iraṇṭum tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa.

iṉip pāṭuvaṉa taliviyiṉ kūṟṟukaḷ:

“naṇṭum ataṉ peṇṭum makiḻntu uṟavāṭum kāṭciyit talivaṉ kāṇkiṟāṉ; piṟaku avaṉ eṉṉi yum pārkkiṟāṉ. avaṉ maṉakkuṟippu yātu? eṉakku viḷaṅkavilli”

“tammuṭiya taṇṇaḷi; tām; avar kutiri pūṭṭiya tēr ivi ellām ippoḻutu eṉṉa āyiṟṟu? emmi avar maṟantuviṭṭār; avar emmiviṭṭu akalalām; yām maṭṭum avari maṟakkavē māṭṭōm; nammi maṟantavari nām maṟakka māṭṭōm”

“neytal pūvē uṉ kaṉaviṉil avar vantāl eṉṉip paṟṟi oru col urikka māṭṭāyā?” “kaṭal aliyē avaṉ kaṭanta vaḻiyiṉ cuvaṭukaḷi ellām nī aḻittu viṭṭāy, nīyum avaṉip pōl koṭiyi, irakkamē illāmal pōyviṭṭatu uṉakku”

“vaḻiyiṉ cuvaṭukaḷi aḻikkum kaṭalē; cōliyē! aṉṉappaṟaviyē! taṇtuṟiyē! avarip pārttu itu takātu eṉṟu kūṟamāṭṭīrā?”

“kaṭal aliyē! avar ceṉṟa tēriṉ cuvaṭukaḷi ēṉ aḻittāy? avarip paṟṟiya niṉivukaḷi aḻippatāl uṉakku eṉṉa naṉmi? nī eṉakkup pakiyākiṉṟāy; uṉṉōṭu iṉi eṉakku uṟavē tēvi illi”

iṉik kūṟuvaṉa tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟukaḷ: “kaṭal cērppaṉē! ivaḷ neñcu maṉmataṉ ampukaḷāl tuḷikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. intap puṇ ataṉi avaḷ aṉṉi aṟintāl avaḷukku yātu kūṟuvatu?”

“mārik kālattup pīrkkampū pōl pūttatu pacali; teyvattiṭam muṟiyiṭṭu innōy tīra vaḻipaṭuvāḷ aṉṉi. yār ikkoṭumi ceytatu eṉṟu kēṭṭāl eṉṉa patil kūṟuvatu?”

“taṉimiyil talivi tuṉpam aṭintu taḷarvāḷ; itaṉi avaḷ vāyviṭṭuk kūṟamuṭiyāmal tavippāḷ, aṉṉikku itaṉi eppaṭi eṭuttuk kūṟa muṭiyum?”

ikkūṟṟukaḷ tōḻi kūṟṟāka amintuḷḷaṉa. mālip poḻutu taliviyi vāṭṭukiṟatu; itē nili talivaṉukku uḷatākumō eṉṟu iṉit talivi kēṭkiṟāḷ.

“mella iruḷ parantatu; cūriyaṉ maṟintuviṭak kaṇkaḷ nīr ukukkiṉṟaṉa; tōḻi! itē mayakkam tarum mālippoḻutu pirintavar uṟiyum nāṭṭil uḷḷatō”

“katiravaṉ maṟintāṉ; kāriruḷ parantatu; eṉ kaṇkaḷ tuṉpak kaṇṇir ukukkiṉṟaṉa; itē pōṉṟa maruḷmāli avar nāṭṭilum uḷḷatō kūṟuvāyāka”

“paṟavikaḷ pāṭṭoli aṭaṅkiviṭṭatu; pakalic ceyyum cūriyaṉ maṟintu viṭṭāṉ; eṉ kaṇkaḷ nīr ukukkiṉṟaṉa. itē mālippoḻutu pirinta avar nāṭṭilum uḷḷatō”

pirinta niliyil avaḷtāṉ aṭiyum tuṉpatti eṭuttuk kūṟukiṟāḷ: “avar emmi maṟakkalām; āṉāl avari yām maṟakka muṭiyātu” eṉṟa karutti vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟukiṟāḷ.

“tāḻi vēli uṭiya uppaṅkaḻikku vantavar emmip poytal viḷiyāṭṭil cantittup paḻaki aḻittuc ceṉṟār. avar nam maṉam viṭṭu akalamāṭṭār”

“nī nalkuka eṉṟu niṉṟavar avar. māṉpōṉṟa maruṇṭa nōkkatti maṟakkamāṭṭār”.

“aṉṉam tam tuṇiyōṭu āṭa ataṉik kaṇṭavar eṉṉiyum nōkkiṉār; eṉṉi varuntac ceytu akalamāṭṭār”

“nāriyē, nī eṉ talivaṉukku eṉ tuṉpa nōyi eṭuttu urikka maṟukkiṟāy; eṉakku nī utavavilli; nī iṅkē vantu eṉṉi aṭiyātu vilakuka, vilakic celka” ivi talivi taṉ tuyaratti eṭuttu urippaṉavākum; kāṉal varippāṭalkaḷ mātavi pāṭi muṭittāḷ.

vēṟu oru putiya paṇṇil toṭarntu cila pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉāḷ. kikkiḷi cērnta cevvaḻip pālip paṇṇip pāṭimuṭitta piṉṉar mālip poḻutu kaṇṭu mayaṅkit talivi terivikkum ceyti kaḷi avaḷ toṭarntu pāṭiṉāḷ.

“pirintavar parivuṭaṉ uritta coṟkaḷi nampi uyir peṟṟu vāḻkiṉṟōm; eṅkaḷ uyiri nī pōkka muṟpaṭukiṟāy. mālippoḻutē! nī eṅkaḷ uyirik koḷvāyā! nī vāḻka”

“pirivut tuyarāl uyir vāḻa muṭiyāmal tavikkiṉṟōm; mēlum eṅkaḷi nī vatikkiṟāy; inta ulakam vāḻum iṭamāka illi; mālippoḻutē! nī tarum tuṉpam mikap peritu; nī vāḻka”

“tīyik kakkikkoṇṭu varukiṟatu. inta mayakkam tarum mālip poḻutu: mākkaṭal teyvamē! uṉṉi muṉṉiṟutti virivil varuvatākac collip pirintār; avar poyc cūḷākiya coṟkaḷi avar kāppāṟṟa muṭiyāmal pōy viṭṭatu; nāṅkaḷ poṟuttuk koḷ kiṟōm. teyvamē nī avari maṉṉittuviṭu; uṉ malar aṭiyi vaṇaṅkukiṟēṉ” - itu taliviyiṉ vēṇṭukōḷ ākiṟatu.

uṟavu muṟital

talivaṉ koṭumi ceytuviṭṭāṉ eṉṟa karuttaminta pāṭalkaḷ kōvalaṉi varuttamuṟac ceytaṉa; “kāṉal varippāṭali yāṉ pāṭiṉēṉ; atil yāṉ entak kuṟippiyum pukuttavilli; avaḷ ivaṟṟi māṟākak karutik kuṟipput tōṉṟap pāṭiviṭṭāḷ; poy pala kūṭṭi urittāḷ ivaḷ” eṉṟu avaṉ karutiṉāṉ; avaḷi veṟuttāṉ. uṟavi muṟittāṉ.

itu eṉṉa avvaḷavu periya tavaṟā? itaṟkākavā ivaṉ avaḷi veṟuppatu? pirivatu? uṟavi aṟuppatu? eṉṟa viṉā eḻalām. yāḻici oru paṟṟuk kōṭāka vittu ūḻviṉi avaḷ vāḻviṉip pāḻpaṭuttiyatu eṉṟutāṉ kūṟamuṭiyum.

pūraṇa nilavu poḻiyum avaḷ aḻakiya mukam; iṉi ati avaṉ kāṇap pōvatilli; tiṅkaḷ mukattāḷ; avaḷik ki nekiḻntu akaṉṟu viṭṭāṉ. aṇippu nīṅkiyatu; piṇippu muṟintatu.

“poḻutu ākiṟatu puṟappaṭuvōm” eṉṟu kūṟi avaḷi aḻikka vēṇṭiyavaṉ avvāṟu kūṟāmal avaḷit taṉiyēviṭṭu ivaṉ maṭṭum ēvalāḷarkaḷ uṭaṉvara avaḷiviṭṭu nīṅkiṉāṉ.

tōḻiyar pēca vāy eṭuttaṉar. avarkaḷip pēcāmal taṭuttāḷ. ōcipaṭāmal eḻuntu paḻiyapaṭi vaṇṭiyuḷ pukuntu vīṭu vantu cērntāḷ. kātalaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟavaḷ tirumpiya pōtu kiyaṟṟa neñcōṭu taṉiyaḷāy vantu cērntāḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷukku atu ēmāṟṟatti aḷittatu.





﻿8. mātavi eḻutiya muṭaṅkal

(vēṉiṟ kāti)

iḷavēṉil paruvam

iḷavēṉil paruvam toṭarntatu. tamiḻakam eṅkum āṭci ceyyum kāmavēḷ ākiya māraṉ avaṉukkut tuṇiyāka iṉiya iḷavēṉil paruvam vantatu. teṉṟalum vīcat toṭaṅkiyatu; kuyil kūviyatu; makaḷiriṉ maṉak kiḷarcciyi ipparuvam eḻuppiyatu.

mātavipāl kōvalaṉ koṇṭa ūṭal avaḷip peritum tākkiyatu; avaḷ taṉimi avaḷi vāṭṭiyatu. taṉ maṉiyakattil avaḷ vēṉiṟ paḷḷiyākiya nilā muṟṟattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkut taṉimiyil yāḻ vācikkat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. vāyviṭṭum pāṭiṉāḷ; avaḷāl anta iciyil īṭupaṭa muṭiyavilli; iciyil avaḷ maṉam nāṭavilli.

mātavi muṭaṅkal

kōvalaṉiṉ niṉivu avaḷi vāṭṭat toṭaṅ kiyatu. avaḷ taṉ kiyakattu irunta pū māliyi eṭuttāḷ. atil palvaki malarkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ tāḻi maṭalit taṉittu eṭuttāḷ. pittiki mukiyi eḻuttāṇiyākak koṇṭāḷ. cempañcuk kuḻampu ataṉi miyākak koṇṭāḷ.

“iḷavēṉil paruvam ākiya iḷavaracaṉ uyirkaḷukku makiḻvu ūṭṭupavaṉ; atōṭu mālippoḻutil tōṉṟum vāṉattut tiṅkaḷ koṭiyatu; tam talivarip pirinta makaḷiri vāṭṭuvatu maṉmataṉ ceyalākum. itaṉi yāṉ uṇarttat tēvi illi; itaṉi aṟintu aruḷvīrāka” eṉṟu vārttikaḷic collik koṇṭu vaṭittu eḻutiṉāḷ. kātal vayappaṭṭa avaḷ maḻalic col pōl vārttikaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭuk kūṟi eḻuti muṭittāḷ. pacanta mēṉiyi uṭiya taṉ tōḻiyākiya vacanta māliyi varuka eṉṟu aḻittu ataṉit tantu aṉuppiṉāḷ. inta muṭaṅkalil colliya ceytikaḷik kōvalaṉukku aṟivittu avaṉi iruntu aḻittu varuka eṉac colli aṉuppiṉāḷ.

vacantamāli ataṉi eṭuttuc ceṉṟu kōvalaṉik kūla maṟuku ākiya kaṭitteruvil cantittut tantāḷ; avaṉ ati vāṅka maṟuttāṉ. mātaviyip paṟṟiya paḻiya niṉivukaḷ avaṉ maṉakkaṇ muṉ vantu mōtiṉa.

kōvalaṉ maṟuppu

avaḷ ceyalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum naṭippē eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. avaḷukku vāḻkkiyē nāṭṭiyak kaliyiṉ kūṟukaḷ eṉṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉāṉ. taṉṉōṭu kātal urikaḷ pēciyatu ‘kaṇ kūṭuvari’ eṉṟāṉ. avaḷ ceytu koṇṭa oppaṉikaḷ ellām, ‘kāṇvarik kōlam’ eṉṟāṉ. ūṭal koṇṭa pōtu aṭaṅkiyavaḷāy ēval makaḷip pōl aṭakka oṭukkamākap paṇintu pēciyatu ‘uḷvariyāṭal’ eṉṟāṉ. taṉṉit taṇivitta poḻutu mutuku puṟam vantu aṇittatu 'puṉ puṟavari' eṉṟāṉ. maṟṟum avaṉ ūṭal ceykikaḷ aṉittum 'kiḷar varikkōlam' eṉṟāṉ; mēlum ‘tērccivari’ ‘kāṭcivari’, ‘eṭuttuk kōḷvari’ eṉṟu peyarkaḷ tantu avaḷ ceyalkaḷi ellām naṭippu eṉṟu kaṭintu kūṟiṉāṉ.

avaḷ āṭal makaḷ ātaliṉ ivi ellām avaḷukkuk kūṭivanta kalikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟic cāṭiṉāṉ. atē pōla inta muṭaṅkalum, ataṉ ceytikaḷum pōlikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟi maṟuttāṉ.

vacanta māli avaḷ eṉṉa ceyyamuṭiyum? vāṭiya uḷḷattuṭaṉ ceṉṟāḷ. mātaviyāl vantu ceytiyi aṟivittāḷ. “iṉṟu māli varāviṭṭālum avari nāḷik kāliyil kāṇpōm” eṉṟu nampikkiyuṭaṉ aṉṟu mātavi tuyiliṉṟik kaḻittāḷ.





9. kōvalaṉ vīṭu tirumputal

(kaṉāttiṟam uritta kāti)

tēvanti aṟimukam

pukārnakaram mālippoḻutu maṅkalamākak kāṭci aḷittuk koṇṭiruntatu; vīṭukaḷ polivuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉa. vīṭṭu muṟṟattil pūvum nellum tūvi aḻakupaṭuttiṉar. vīṭukaḷukku viḷakkēṟṟi vittaṉar. anta mālip poḻutu tēvanti eṉṉum pārppaṉap peṇ kaṇṇakikku āṟutal kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ.

intat tēvanti vāḻkkippaṭṭa vīṭu oru piṉṉaṇiyik koṇṭu iruntatu. ivaḷi maṇantatu pācāṇṭac cāttaṉ eṉṉum teyvam; anta vīṭṭil atteyvam ākiya cāttaṉ vaḷarntu vayatukku vantu uriya vālipapparuvattil ivaḷimaṇam ceytu koṇṭāṉ. itu vicittiramāṉa kati.

mālati eṉpavaḷ orutti taṉ māṟṟāḷiṉ kuḻantikkup pāl ūṭṭa atu vikkic cettatu. antap paḻi taṉ mītu varum eṉṟu añci avaḷ ūril uḷḷa pala kōyilkaḷukkum ceṉṟu akkuḻantiyik kiṭatti uyirppittut tarumāṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ. piṉpu pācaṇṭac cāttaṉukku uriya kōyili aṭintāḷ. aṅkē avaḷ varam vēṇṭip pāṭu kiṭantāḷ.

aṅkē iṭupiṇam tiṉṉum iṭākiṉip pēy oṉṟu vantu, “tavam ceyyātavarkkut tēvarkaḷ varam taramāṭṭarkaḷ” eṉṟu kūṟic cetta kuḻantiyik kiyāl paṟṟic cuṭukāṭṭukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟu taṉ vayiṟṟu akattu iṭṭu viḻuṅki viṭṭatu.

uyir nīṅkiya uṭalum aṭiyāḷattukkuk kūṭa illāmāl pōy viṭṭatu. iṭi uṇṭa mayil pōl tuṭituṭittāḷ, ēṅki aḻutāḷ.

“aṉṉiyē nī aḻātē” eṉṟu kūṟip pakkattil oru cōliyil kiṭanta oru kuḻantiyik kāṭṭi “eṭuttuc celka” eṉṟu atteyvam kūṟiyatu. pācāṇṭac cāttaṉē akkuḻanti vaṭivākiyatu. māṟṟuk kuḻanti kiṭittavuṭaṉ ataṉi vīṭṭukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟu taṉ paḻiyip pōkkik koṇṭāḷ.

antak kuḻanti vaḷarntu kalvi peṟṟup periyavaṉ āki antak kuṭumpattup poṟuppukaḷi ēṟṟu vālipaṉ āyiṉāṉ. tāyattārōṭu cottu urimikku vaḻakkut toṭuttu appeṟṟōrkaḷ maṟinta piṟaku avarkaḷukku uriya kaṭamikaḷic ceytu maṇamum ceytu koṇṭāṉ. tēvanti eṉpāḷ avaṉ vāḻkkit tuṇiviyāyiṉāḷ.

nāḷkaḷ cila nakarntaṉa. avaḷōṭu nīṭittu vāḻāmal tāṉ teyvam eṉpati avaḷukku uṇartti “emmuṭiya kōyilukku nī vā” eṉṟu kūṟic ceṉṟāṉ; ūravarkkup patil kūṟa vēṇṭum. tēcāntiram ceṉṟa kaṇavaṉ tirumpi varuvataṟkākat tāṉ kōyil ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭuvatākak kūṟi vaḻipāṭukaḷ mēṟkoṇṭu vantāḷ.

kaṇṇaki kaṉavu

kaṇṇaki taṉ kaṇavaṉip pirintu kavalkiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu aṟintu avaḷukku āṟutal kūṟa avaḷ illam aṭintāḷ. pūvum nellum tūvik kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭuk kaṇṇaki taṉ kaṇavaṉi aṭivāḷāka eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ. ataṉi avaḷukku aṟivuṟuttiṉāḷ. eppaṭiyum kaṇavaṉi aṭivēṉ eṉṟu kaṇṇaki taṉ maṉa uṟutiyit terivittāḷ. ataṉōṭu tāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉaviṉi avaḷukku eṭuttu urittāḷ.

“antak kaṉaviṉi niṉittālē eṉ neñcam naṭuṅkukiṉṟatu. eṉ kaṇavaṉ eṉ kiyip piṭittup putiya ūrukku aḻittuc celkiṟāṉ. aṅkē paḻic col oṉṟu kūṟiṉar. tēḷit tūkki eṅkaḷ mēl pōṭṭatu pōl atu iruntatu. ataṉit toṭarntu kōvalaṉukku oru tīṅku nikaḻntatāl atu kēṭṭuk kāvalaṉ muṉ ceṉṟu vaḻakkāṭa ataṉit toṭarntu annakarum aracaṉum aḻivu peṟṟaṉar. ataṉ piṉ uyir tuṟanta eṉ talivaṉ uyir peṟṟu eṉṉic cantittup pēciṉār. itaṉi nī nampamāṭṭāy; cirittup poy eṉṟu kūṟuvāy; itu yāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉavu ākum” eṉṟu urittāḷ.

tēvantiyiṉ aṟivuri

ataṉik kēṭṭa tēvanti, “uṉ kaṇavaṉ uṉṉi veṟukkavilli. ceṉṟa piṟaviyil nī uṉ kaṇavaṉukkākac ceyya vēṇṭiya nōṉpiṉic ceyyāmal viṭṭu viṭṭāy. ataṉi ippoḻutu nīkkuka. kaṭaloṭu kāviri kalakkum caṅkamattuṟiyil neytal maṇakkum kāṉalil cōmakuṇṭam cūriya kuṇṭam eṉṉum nīrttaṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. annīrt tuṟikaḷil muḻukik kāmavēḷ ākiya maṉmataṉ kōyil ceṉṟu toḻutāl inta ulakattil peṇkaḷ kaṇavaṉōṭu iṉpuṟṟu vāḻvar; aṭutta piṟaviyilum iruvarum pōkam cey poṉṉulakil ceṉṟu piṟappar. nām iruvarum ceṉṟu nīrāṭuvōm” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ.

avaḷ uritta urikaḷ aṉittum cevi koṭuttuk kēṭṭa kaṇṇaki orē collil takka viṭi tantu avaḷ vāyi aṭakkiṉāḷ. “pīṭu aṉṟu” eṉṟu kūṟi avaḷi aṉuppi vittāḷ.

ataṉpiṉ ēvaṟpeṇ orutti yārō varukiṟārkaḷ eṉpatu aṟintu kaṭittali ceṉṟu pārttāḷ. vantavaṉ kāvalaṉ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭāḷ. anta vīṭṭut talivaṉ eṉpati aṟivittāḷ.

kōvalaṉum nērē vantu avaḷ perumimikka paḷḷiyaṟikkuc ceṉṟu avaḷip pārttāṉ; vāṭiya mēṉikaṇṭu varuttam aṭintāṉ; avaṉ avaḷōṭu uriyāṭiya coṟkaḷ poruḷ potintaviyāka iruntaṉa. “vañcaṉi mikka oruttiyiṉ valiyil akappaṭṭuk kulam tanta celvatti ellām tolittu viṭṭēṉ. kuṉṟu pōṉṟa celvam atu kuṉṟi viṭṭatu; ippoḻutu vaṟumi vantu uḷḷatu. ilampāṭu atu eṉakku nāṇattit tarukiṟatu” eṉṟu avaḷiṭam taṉ maṉaniliyit terivittāṉ; ceṟivu mikka coṟkaḷik kēṭṭu aṟivu mikka kaṇṇaki nalamtikaḻ muṟuval nakimukam kāṭṭic “cilampu uḷa koḷka” eṉṟāḷ. ic coṟkaḷ avaṉukku eḻucci tantaṉa. avaṉ cintaṉi cīrpeṟṟup paṟantatu. avaḷōṭu kalantu uriyāṭi avaḷ kūṟum karuttukku avaṉ kāttiruntāṉ. “iḻanta poruḷi īṭṭutal vēṇṭum, intac cilampiṉi mutaṟ poruḷākak koṇṭu nakikaḷ celvam aṉittiyum mīṭpēṉ” eṉṟāṉ. atōṭu avaṉ niṟuttik koḷḷavilli. māṭamaturikkuc ceṉṟu vāṇipam ceyyum karutti aṟivittāṉ.

ataṉōṭu avaṉ amiyavilli. avaḷiyum ‘uṭaṉ varuka’ eṉṟu aḻittāṉ. ivvaḷavu virivāka muṭivu ceytatu viyappākattāṉ uḷḷatu. avaṉ eṉṉa ceyvāṉ? ūḻviṉi avaṉi āṭṭuvittatu. iruḷ nīṅkiya viṭiyaṟpoḻutil arumaṉi viṭṭu akaṉṟāṉ.

kaṇṇakiyuṭaṉ avaṉ puṟappaṭṭāṉ. mātavi avaṉ kāli varuvāṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyatu paḻutu ākiviṭṭatu. yār iti etirpārttārkaḷ? nampikki avaḷip poṟutta vari taḷarntatu. avaḷ vāḻkki cōkattil āḻntatu. tuṉpam avaḷip paṟṟiyatu.





10. cōḻa nāṭṭup payaṇam

(nāṭu kāṇ kāti)

veḷiyē eḻutal

katiravaṉ eḻuvataṟku muṉ viṭiyaṟkāliyil iruvarum eḻuntu puṟappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ virumpiyā puṟappaṭṭaṉar? illi; ūḻviṉi avarkaḷi iyakkiyatu. avarkaḷ vāḻviṉi māṟṟiyamittatu.

vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiṉar. nīṇṭa kataviṉi uṭiya peruvāyil; māperum māḷiki, maṟṟum katavi mūṭiṉar; ataṉ ciṟpa vēlippāṭukaḷ poṟpuṭaṉ tikaḻntaṉa. maṟakka muṭiyāta ōviyaṅkaḷ; āṭṭukkiṭāy, kavarimāṉ; aṉṉappaṟavi immūṉṟum muṟṟattil naṭpuṭaṉ tirintu koṇṭiruntaṉa. palamuṟi avaṟṟip pārttu makiḻntavarkaḷ avarkaḷ; avaṟṟi viṭṭu vara maṉam iyiyavilli; maṟupaṭiyum avaṟṟip pārttuviṭṭu akaṉṟaṉar.

vīṭṭumuṉ nīṇṭa vaḻippāti, ataṉi neṭuṅkaḻi eṉṟaṉar. ataṉik kaṭantu ūrppuṟatti aṭintaṉar. aṅkē avarkaḷ tirumāliṉ tirukkōyilik kaṭantavarāki ataṉ valappakkam ceṉṟaṉar. aṭuttatu puttapaḷḷi; aṅkē antarattiliruntu muṉivarkaḷ vantu taṅki aṟivu upatēcam ceyvatu vaḻakkam. ataṉik kaṭantu aṭuttu irunta camaṇar cilātalattiyum kaṇṭavarāki ataṉiyum kaṭantaṉar. ataṉ piṉ ūrukku veḷiyē irunta peruvaḻi; ataṉ vaḻiyē naṭantu ilavantiki eṉṉum cōliyiṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu, kāviriyiṉ vaṭakkup pakkam kavunti aṭikaḷ taṅki irunta camaṇap paḷḷiyi aṭintaṉar. appaḷḷiyiṉ matiṟpuṟattil ciṟitu taṅki ōyvu eṭuttaṉar. kāvatam tūramē avarkaḷ kaṭantaṉar. ataṟkuḷ kaṇṇaki kaḷippuṟṟāḷ. iṉṉum ciṟitu tūrattilēyē maturi irukkum eṉṟu avaḷ eṇṇic cirittuk koṇṭē, “maturi mutūr eṅkē uḷḷatu” eṉṟu viḷittuk kēṭṭāḷ. aṉupavamillāta peṇ; melliya coṟkaḷ; avaṉum “kāvatam muppatutāṉ” eṉṟu mika eḷitāka eṭuttu urittāṉ. mikavum arukilēyē uḷḷatu eṉṟu avaḷukkuc ciramam tōṉṟātu irukkak kūṟiṉāṉ. muraṇcuvi aminta uriyāṭal avarkaḷukku ayarvu ciṟitu pōkkiyatu. kavunti aṭikaḷ cantippu

avarkaḷukkup pēccuttuṇi tēvippaṭṭatu. avarkaḷ mutaṉ mutalil cantittavar kavunti aṭikaḷ; avar oru camaṇattuṟavi, avarkaḷ taṅki irunta paḷḷi iruntatu. avarik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkit toḻutaṉar.

ivarkaḷ tōṟṟam avarkaḷ ēṟṟattik kāṭṭiyatu. avarkaḷ cīlamum, iṟippaṟṟum uṭiyavarkaḷ eṉpatik kavunti aṭikaḷāl aṟiya muṭintatu. ‘uruvum, kulaṉum, uyarpēr oḻukkamum, perumakaṉ tirumoḻi piṟaḻā nōṉpum uṭiyavar eṉpati eṭuttuk kūṟi uṟṟa tuyar yātu’ eṉṟu viṉaviṉār. vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟak kāraṇam yātu eṉpatu avar kēṭṭa kēḷviyāka amintatu.

kōvalaṉum tāṉ yār? ēṉ iṅkuvantāṉ? intap paḻaṅkatikaḷip pēcāmal “poruḷ īṭṭutaṟku” eṉṟu curukkamākak kūṟiṉāṉ. avarkaḷ maṉa uṟutiyik kaṇṭu teḷinta kavunti aṭikaḷ avarkaḷ vaḻippayaṇattit taṭuttu niṟutta muyalavilli: avaravar pōkkukaḷi avaravarē vakuppatu takkatu; maṟṟavarkaḷ iṭiniṉṟu taṭuppatu tavaṟu eṉpati naṉku aṟintavarākiya kavunti aṭikaḷ avarkaḷ celvatu maturi eṉpati aṟintār.

avarukkum nīṇṭa nāḷ vēṭki; teṉtamiḻ nāṭṭut tītutīr maturikkuc cellavēṇṭum eṉṟa viruppam iruntatu. aṅkē camaṇaneṟi paṟṟi mēlum aṟintu koḷḷa āval koṇṭiruntār. aṟiñarkaḷ urikaḷik kēṭṭu aṟiyum āval taṉakku uḷḷatākak kūṟit tāṉum uṭaṉ varuvatāka urittār.

paḻam naḻuvip pālil viḻuntatu. tēṉiṉum iṉitāka accoṟkaḷ kōvalaṉukku icittaṉa. “aṭikaḷē! nīrum varuvatāyiṉ kaṇṇakiyiṉ kaṭuntuyar tīrntatu. takka tuṇi eṅkaḷukkuk kiṭittatu” eṉṟu patil urittāṉ. kavunti aṭikaḷum uṭaṉ puṟappaṭa uṭaṉpaṭṭār.

vaḻikaḷik kūṟutal

aṟivumikkavar; maturi paṟṟi naṉku aṟintavar; pōkum vaḻikaḷiyum aṟintu vittavar; ataṉāl kavuntiyaṭikaḷ vaḻiyiṉ arumiyit tāṉ terinta aḷavu avarkaḷukku urittār. celvataṟku uriya vaḻikaḷ iraṇṭu; cōli vaḻiyē celvatu; maṟṟoṉṟu vayal vaḻiyē celvatu; iraṇṭilum uḷḷa taṭikaḷ, iṭiyūṟukaḷ evi eṉpati eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.

“veyyiliṉ koṭumi tīrac cōli vaḻiyē celvatu takkatutāṉ; eṉṟālum avvaḻiyil kuṟikaḷ uḷa; avaṟṟiyum aṟivīrāka” eṉṟār.

“kiḻaṅkukaḷi akaḻntu eṭuttuk kuḻikaḷ uṇṭākkuvar. aṭuttuḷḷa ceṇpaka maraṅkaḷ tam pūkkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ mītu paṭiya avaṟṟi maṟittuviṭum. pūkkaḷ tāṉē eṉṟu aṭi eṭuttu vittāl kuḻikaḷ avi; viḻuntāl eḻuvatu aritu, cari, avaṟṟiyum kaṇittu aṇittu uḷḷa palāmaraṅkaḷ ceṟinta pāti ceṉṟāl ataṉ kaṉikaḷ naṭappāriṉ talikaḷi muṭṭum. avaṟṟi vilakki mañcaḷum iñciyum mayaṅkik kiṭakkum pāttikaḷil ceṉṟāl aṅkē palāviṉ koṭṭikaḷ kūḻāṅkal pōl taṭuttu uṟuttum.

cōlivaḻi ataṉit tavirttu vēṟuvayal vaḻitēṭuvōm eṉṟāl niḻal tarum maraṅkaḷ illāta pāti; eṅkum vayalkaḷ; aṅkē kuvaḷikaḷ pūkkum nīrnilikaḷ. avaṟṟil uḷḷa vāḷimīṉik kavva nīrnāy cellum; avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tappa avvāḷikaḷ naṭakkum pātiyil tuḷḷi vantu viḻum; ataṉik kaṇṭu inta melliyalāḷ tiṭukkiṭak kūṭum. tīmi nikaḻātu; eṉiṉum atik kaṇṭu kaṇṇaki añcum nili ēṟpaṭa vāyppu uḷḷatu. karumpil viḷinta tēṉ cāṟu aṭuttu uḷḷa poykiyic cārum; nīr eṉṟu aḷḷik kuṭittāl atu karumpiṉ cāṟu ākaliṉ atu mayakkattit tarak kūṭum; ataṉāl kaṇṇaki varunta nēriṭum; maṟṟum karuviḷi malarkaḷik kaḷipaṟippār avaṟṟi vaḻiyil pōṭuvar; avaṟṟil vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum; karuviḷi malartāṉē eṉṟu kālaṭi vittāl vaṇṭukaḷ nacuṅki varuntum; naṇṭum nantum kālaṭikaḷil nacuṅkic cātalum kūṭum; uyirkaḷukku ūṟu ceyyum iṭiyūṟu ēṟpaṭa vāyppu uḷḷatu” eṉṟu vayal vaḻiyil uḷḷa kuṟikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.

muvarum maturi ēkutal

cōliyā vayalā avarkaḷ cintaṉikku viṭṭu viṭṭuk kavuntiyaṭikaḷ payaṇattukkup puṟappaṭṭār. kaṭṭuc cōṟum peṭṭip paṭukkiyumā eṭuttuc cella muṭiyum? tōḷil toṅkaviṭṭa uṟi; atil oru cōṟu uṇṇum piccip pāttiram; maṟṟum kiyil oru mayiṟ pīli; uyirkaḷukku ūṟu nikaḻātapaṭi taṭukka anta mayiṟpīli; tuṟavikku iviyē cottukaḷ; ivaṟṟi ēntiya niliyil avarkaḷōṭu kavuntiyaṭikaḷ puṟappaṭṭār.

vaḻinaṭik kāṭcikaḷ

kāvirik kariyil avarkaḷ payaṇam toṭarntatu; avarkaḷ ceṉṟa vaḻi vayalkaḷ mikku uḷḷa veḷikaḷē ākiyatu; uḻavarkaḷ eḻuppiya ōci avarkaḷi makiḻvittatu; kāviri nīr iṭiyaṟātu ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu. ataṉ calacalappu avarkaḷukkuk kalakalappit tantatu. nīr iṟikka av uḻavarkaḷ ēṟṟaṅkaḷiyum, nīrk kūṭikaḷiyum tēṭavilli; āṟṟunīr vāykkāl vaḻi ōṭi vayalkaḷil pāyntatu. ataṉ oli cevikku iṉimi tantatu. vayalkaḷil cennellum, ceṅkarumpum ceḻittu vaḷarntaṉa. ivvayalkaḷi aṭutta kayaṅkaḷil tāmarikaḷ pūttu viḷaṅkiṉa. attaṭaṅkaḷilum, aṭuttuḷḷa cōlikaḷilum nīrp paṟavikaḷ pala kural eḻuppiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavi kampuḷ kōḻi, nāri, aṉṉam, kokku, kāṭṭukkōḻi, nīrkkākki, uḷḷāṉ, ūral, puḷ, putā maṟṟum nīrppaṟavikaḷ pala ākum. ivi kūṭi eḻuppiya kural pōrk kaḷattu vīrarkaḷ eḻuppum palvēṟu vaki olikaḷākak kalantu olittatu. aṅkē eṉṉa? atu pōrkkaḷāmā? viyattaku kāṭciyāka iruntatu; pēririccal kēṭpataṟku iṉitāka viḷaṅkiyatu.

uḻavarkaḷ illaṅkaḷ aṅkaṅkē avarkaḷ eḻuppiya āravāra olikaḷ kēṭpataṟku iṉimi tantatu. cēṟṟil puraṇṭu eḻunta erumi taṉ tiṉavip pōkkik koḷḷa neṟ kūṭukaḷil vantu urāya akkūṭukaḷil koṭṭi vitta tāṉiya vakikaḷ neṟkatirkaḷil cinta antak kāṭciyik kaṇṭu kiviṉit toḻilarum, uḻavar perumakkaḷum uvaki koṇṭu āravārittaṉar. atu iṉpa oliyāka icittatu.

uḻattiyarkaḷ vayalkaḷattil pukuntu kaḷi paṟittaṉar; nāṟṟu naṭṭaṉar; cēṟupaṭinta kōlam avarkaḷi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭiyatu. uḻitta kaḷipput tīrat taḻitta kaḷḷip parukik kaḷi makiḻvu koṇṭu putup putuppāṭalkaḷip pāṭi makiḻntaṉar. ammakiḻvup pāṭalkaḷ kēṭpōri mayaṅka vittaṉa. intap putumi kaṇṭu avarkaḷ putumakiḻvu kaṇṭaṉar.

uḻukiṉṟa kalappikkup pūkkaḷ cūṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭup piṉ ataṉik koṇṭu nilatti uḻutaṉar; avarkaḷ ekkaḷippuk koṇṭu pāṭiya pāṭal ‘ēr maṅkalam’ eṉappaṭṭatu. arintu kuvittuk kaṭāviṉiviṭṭu atari tirittu nelli muṟam koṇṭu tūṟṟiyapōtu pāṭiya pāṭṭu ‘mukavip pāṭṭu’ eṉappaṭṭatu. kiṇip porunar muḻavu koṇṭu eḻuppiyatu ‘kiṇi ici’ eṉappaṭṭatu. immūvakip pāṭalkaḷik kēṭṭuk koṇṭē vaḻinaṭi varuttam teriyāmal naṭantu ceṉṟaṉar.

vaḻiyil pala ūrkaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉar. maṟiyavarkaḷ vēḷvikaḷ eḻuppiya puki, uḻavarkaḷ karumpu ālikaḷil mūṭṭiya tīyil tōṉṟiya puki vāṉatti mūṭiṉa; avi mali tavaḻum mēkam eṉak kaviḻntiruntaṉa; ittakiya antaṇarkaḷ irukkikaḷum, uḻavarkaḷ illaṅkaḷum uṭiya ūrkaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉar. nāḷ oṉṟukku oru karvatamē avarkaḷāl kaṭakka muṭintatu. aṅkaṅkē iruḷ kavvum nēraṅkaḷil cōlikaḷil taṅki ōyvu peṟṟaṉar; kaḷippu āṟiṉar.

ūrkaḷ pala kaṭantavar tiruvaraṅkatti aṭutta oru cōliyil taṅkiṉar. aṅkē ulaka nōṉpikaḷ iṭṭa cilātalam oṉṟu iruntatu; paṭṭiṉappākkattu uyarkuṭi makkaḷ amitta cilātalam atuvākum. aṅkē aṟivum, ñāṉamum mikka camaṇar talivarkaḷ vantu taṅkuvatu uṇṭu; avarkaḷic cāraṇar eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ mata kuru āvārkaḷ.

immūvarum ceṉṟapōtu cāraṇar oruvar aṅku vantu antac cilātalattil amarntiruntār. avar camaṇa mata kuruvākak karutappaṭṭavar. ivar viṇvaḻiyē vantu maṇṇakattu āṅkāṅku iruntu aruḷ ceytār; atu avar arumpaṇiyāka iruntatu.

cāraṇar perumakaṉārik kaṇṭa immūvarum avar tiruvaṭikaḷil viḻuntu vaṇaṅkiṉar. immūvarik kaṇṭa accāraṇar ivarkaḷ cōkak katikaḷik kēṭka muṟpaṭavilli; kaṇṭatum ivarkaḷ nili yātu eṉpati aṟinta ñāṉi avar; ittuyaraṅkaḷi nēriṭi kaḷiya muṟpaṭavilli.

avaravarkaḷ tam viṉikaḷukku ēṟpa viḷivukaḷi aṭivatu iyalpu eṉpati naṉku aṟintavarākat tikaḻntār. avar viruppu veṟuppu aṟṟa maṉanili koṇṭavar ātaliṉ ivarkaḷik kaṇṭu enta varuttamum koṇṭatākak kāṭṭik koḷḷavilli.

avar kavunti aṭikaḷukku aṟavuri kūṟiṉār. mūṉṟu pēruṇmikaḷi avar kavunti aṭikaḷukku urittu aruḷ ceytār. “ūḻviṉi uruttu vantu ūṭṭum” eṉṟum, “vāḻkki niliyaṟṟatu” eṉṟum, “piṟappu aṟukka arukaṉ tiruvaṭi vaṇaṅka vēṇṭum” eṉṟum aṟavuri kūṟiṉār.

kavunti aṭikaḷ perumakiḻvu koṇṭu iṟip pēraruḷi viyantu pōṟṟiṉār. arukaṉip pukaḻntu pēciṉār. arukaṉiṉ tirunāmaṅkaḷic colli vāḻttiṉār. cāraṇar perumakaṉār avarukkup ‘piṟavi nīṅkuka; pācam aṟuka” eṉṟu āci kūṟi accilātalam viṭṭu eḻuntu ēkiṉār. aṅkē oru viyattaku kāṭci nikaḻntatu. avar antarattil cilātalattukku mēl oru muḻam uyara niṉṟār. atu putumimikkatāka iruntatu. ataṉpiṉ avar viṇṇil uyarntu vāṉvaḻic ceṉṟu maṟintār. ēṉiya iruvarum accāraṇarit toḻutu vaṇaṅkiṉar. tam piṟavip piṇiyum tīrka eṉa vēṇṭik koṇṭaṉar.

ataṉ piṉ kāviri nīrt tuṟiyi aṭuttup pūviricōli oṉṟil taṅkiṉar; aṅku avviruvarum kaḷittu ayarntu iruntaṉar. ayalil kavunti aṭikaḷ tuṇi iruntaṉar.

iḷaṅ kātalarkaḷ ivarkaḷ kāmaṉum ratiyumākap putiyavarkaḷukkuk kāṭci aḷittaṉar. paratti oruttiyum, avaḷ kātalaṉ veṟṟuriyāḷaṉ oruvaṉum avvaḻiyē vantaṉar; avarkaḷ viḻikaḷil ivarkaḷ paṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ pēraḻakil mayaṅkiya avarkaḷ, “ivarkaḷ yār?” eṉṟu anta mutiya tavamātiṉik kēṭṭaṉar. “makkaḷ kāṇīr! mikka tuyaramuṟṟaṉar; avarkaḷi viṭṭu vilakuvīr” eṉṟār.

“makkaḷ” eṉṟāl oru tāy vayiṟṟil piṟantavar eṉṟa poruḷil eṭuttuk koṇṭu “oru tāy vayiṟṟup piṟantavar kātal ceyvatum uṇṭō?” eṉṟu eḷḷi nakiyāṭiṉar. itaṉit tāṅkātu kavunti aṭikaḷ vekuṇṭu avarkaḷic capittār. eḷḷip pēciya avar kaḷi “muḷḷuṭik kāṭṭil mutunariyākuka” eṉṟu cāpamiṭṭār.

avarkaḷ narikaḷāka māṟi ūḷiyiṭṭaṉar. inta māṟṟattik kaṇṭu kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum tiṭukkiṭṭaṉar. perumi mikka kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum añci irakkam koṇṭu avarkaḷukkākap parintu pēciṉar. “neṟiyil nīṅkiyavarkaḷ aṟiyāmiyāl iḻitta tavaṟukaḷ ivi avarkaḷ piḻittup pōvatu takkatu: avarkaḷi maṉṉikkavum” eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉā.

“yāṇṭu oṉṟu avarkaḷ uṟiyūriṉi aṭutta kāṭṭil uṟivar; piṉ avarkaḷ taṉ paḻiya uru peṟuvar” eṉṟu kūṟi avarkaḷ cāpatti māṟṟiṉār. ataṉpiṉ ivarkaḷ tām taṅki irunta cōliyi viṭṭuc cōḻar tam talinakarākiya uṟantiyi aṭintaṉar. av uṟiyūrukkuk ‘kōḻiyūr’ eṉṟa peyarum akkālattil vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṟku oru kati piṉṉaṇiyāka viḷaṅkiyatu. yāṉiyi oru cēvaṟ kōḻi veṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. vīrattiṟku itu oru eṭuttukkāṭṭāka viḷaṅkiyatu; tiruvaraṅkatti aṭuttu avarkaḷ uṟiyūr pōyc cērntaṉar. atu cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ teṉ elliyāka iruntatu.





﻿2. maturik kāṇṭam



11. kāṭukaḷik kaṭattal

(kāṭukāṇ kāti)

mūṉṟu kuṭikaḷi aṭukki vittatu pōṉṟa mukkuṭik kīḻ ataṉ kuḷir niḻalil uṟiyum tēvaṉ ākiya arukaṉ; avaṉ ñāyiṟu pōṉṟa oḷi paṭittavaṉ; acōka marattu niḻalil amarntavaṉ; avaṉ tiruvaṭi vaṇaṅkiṉar. piṉpu vaḻipāṭu muṭittu avūril nikkantaṉ paḷḷi eṉṟa aṟappaḷḷiyi aṭintaṉar. aṅkē camaṇat tuṟavikaḷ cilar taṅki iruntaṉar. āṟṟiṭik kuṟiyākiya tiruvaraṅkattil cāraṇar peru makaṉpāl kēṭṭu aṟinta aṟamoḻikaḷik kavunti aṭikaḷ avarkkuc ceppiṉār. attuṟavikaḷ taṅkiyirunta maṭattilēyē ammūvarum or iravu taṅkiṉar.

maṟunāḷ kāliyil uṟiyūriviṭṭut teṉtici nōkkic cellap puṟappaṭṭu vikaṟi yāmattil uṟi yūr elliyik kaṭantu vaḻiyil oru cōliyakattut taṅkiṉar.

māṅkāṭṭu maṟiyavaṉ

appoḻutu pāṇṭiyaṉ pukaḻip paṟi cāṟṟi yavaṉāy oru māmaṟiyāḷaṉ avarkaḷ muṉ vantu cērntāṉ. avaṉi nōkki “num ūr yātu? iṅku varuvataṉ kāraṇam eṉṉa?” eṉṟu kōvalaṉ viṉaviṉāṉ.

avaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil iruntu vantavaṉ eṉpati avaṉ kūṟṟu niṟuviyatu. pāṇṭiyar tam paṇṭip perumikaḷi aṭukkik kūṟiṉāṉ. kaṭal alikaḷ karikaḷil mōta avaṟṟil kāl vittu ataṉi aṭakkiṉāṉ oru pāṇṭiyaṉ; avaṉ vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ ataṉi vaṟṟac ceyvataṟkut taṉ kivēlik kaṭalil eṟiya atu poṟukkātu kaṭalkōḷ eḻuntu paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟiyum kumarik kōṭu eṉṉum maliyiyum aḻittuviṭṭatu. elli curuṅkiya kāraṇattāl avaṉ ataṉip perukka vaṭanāṭu paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu kaṅkiyiyum, imayattiyum taṉ aṭippaṭuttiṉāṉ. itu oru varalāṟu.

maṟṟoru pāṇṭiyaṉ intiraṉāl pārāṭṭappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ tanta poṉṉārattit taṉ kaḻuttil pūṇṭāṉ. 'āram pūṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ' eṉa avaṉ pārāṭṭap peṟṟāṉ.

maṟṟoru pāṇṭiyaṉ intiraṉi etirttu avaṉ talimuṭiyit taṉ kic ceṇṭāl aṭittuc citaṟac ceytāṉ. ataṉāl intiraṉ vekuṇṭu eḻuntu eḻil mikka mēkaṅkaḷi maḻi poḻiyāmal taṭuttāṉ; avaṟṟic ciṟippiṭittu iḻuttu vantu niṟimaḻi poḻiyumāṟu pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭṭaḷi iṭṭāṉ. ‘mēkattik kāl taḷiyiṭṭup paṇivitta pāṇṭiyaṉ ivaṉ’ eṉṟu pārāṭṭappaṭṭāṉ, icceyalic ceytavaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti eṉpāṉ āvāṉ. im mūvariyum vāḻka eṉṟu vāḻttuk kūṟiyavaṉāy ammaṟiyavaṉ vantāṉ. pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ pukaḻ moḻikaḷi aṭukkik kūṟiya piṉ avaṉ taṉ payaṇattip paṟṟi oru virivuriyiyē nikaḻttiṉāṉ.

“tiruvaraṅkattil tirumāl tuyilum vaṇṇattiyum, tiruvēṅkaṭattil atteyvam niṟkum aḻakiṉiyum kāṇap puṟappaṭṭēṉ. yāṉ kuṭamali māṅkāṭṭil vacittu varupavaṉ, teṉṉavaṉ nāṭṭiṉ tītu tīr ciṟappi nēril kaṇṭu varukiṟēṉ; ataṉāltāṉ avaṉi vāḻttik koṇṭu varukiṟēṉ; ituvē eṉ varuki” eṉṟu av antaṇaṉ urittāṉ. tēcaṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭa avaṉiṭam kōvalaṉ nēcam pārāṭṭiṉāṉ.

“māmaṟi mutalvaṉē! maturikkuc cellum neṟiyātu? ataṉik kūṟuka” eṉṟu kōvalaṉ kēṭka avaṉ vaḻittiṟattit taṉ nāvaṉmi tōṉṟak kūṟiṉāṉ.

mūṉṟu vaḻikaḷ

“nīṅkaḷ intak kaṭum veyyilil kāriki ivaḷuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭu vantuḷaḷīr kuṟiñciyum mulliyum tam nalliyalpu tirintu pāli eṉṟu innilap pakutikaḷ vaṭivam koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. inta neṭuvaḻiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl koṭumpāḷūr eṉṉum ūril neṭuṅkuḷam oṉṟu varum; aṅkuc civaṉ ēnti niṟkum cūlattip pōla ataṉ kariyil mūṉṟu vaḻikaḷ kiḷikkiṉṟaṉa.

valatu puṟam

itaṉ valap pakkam ceṉṟāl marām, omi, uḻiñcil, mūṅkil, kāyntu kiṭakkum maram mutaliyaṉa uḷḷa kāṭu uḷḷatu. nīr vēṭkiyāl māṉkaḷ kūvi viḷikkum kāṭṭiyum, eyiṉar kuṭiyiruppiyum kaṭantāl aivaṉam eṉṉum nellum, karumpum, tiṉiyum, varakum, veḷḷuppum, mañcaḷum, kavalik koṭiyum, vāḻi, kamuku, teṅku, mā, palāvum cūḻnta teṉṉavaṉ ciṟumali tōṉṟum. ammaliyi valamākak koṇṭu ceṉṟāl maturiyampatiyi aṭiyalām” eṉṟāṉ. iṭap puṟam

“avvaḻi cellāmal iṭappakkam ceṉṟāl vayal kaḷum cōlikaḷum uṭiya vaḻiyik kaṭantāl tirumāl kuṉṟatti aṭivīr. aṅkē mayakkam keṭukkum pila vaḻi oṉṟu uṇṭu. ip pila vaḻiyil ceṉṟāl puṇṇiya caravaṇam, pavakāraṇi iṭṭacitti eṉṟa peyar peṟṟa mūṉṟu poykikaḷik kāṇa muṭiyum”.

“avaṟṟuḷ puṇṇiya caravaṇam ākiya poykiyil nīrāṭiṉāl intiraṉ eḻutiya aintiram eṉṉum ilakkaṇatti aṟintavar āvīr”.

“pavakāraṇi eṉṉum poykiyil nīrāṭiṉāl intap piṟavikkuk kāraṇamāka irukkum paḻam piṟappip paṟṟi aṟivīr”.

“iṭṭacittiyil nīrāṭiṉāl nīṅkaḷ karutum poruḷ aṉittum peṟuvīr. eṉavē ippilattuḷ ceṉṟu ētōṉum oṉṟil muḻuka vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutiṉāl tirumāl kuṉṟattil aṅku viṟṟirukkum tirumāli mutalil vaṇaṅkuvīrāka. attirumālit tutittu viṭṭu ammaliyi mummuṟi valam vantāl aṅkē cilampāṟṟaṅ kariyil malar pūttavēṅki marattu niḻalil oru peṇ teyvam vantu tōṉṟum. atteyvam mūṉṟu viṉākkaḷit toṭukkum. immikku iṉpam taruvatu yātu? maṟumikku iṉpam taruvatu yātu? ivviraṇṭum allāmal eppoḻutum nilittu niṟkum iṉpam yātu? eṉṟa viṉākkaḷi eḻuppum; ivaṟṟiṟkut takka viṭi tantāl pilattukkuḷḷē cellum takuti uṇṭu eṉṟu atteyvam uṟuti ceyyum. avarkaḷukku uḷḷē cella vaḻi kāṭṭum; avaḷ ammaliyil nilittu vāḻpavaḷ; malic celvi eṉṟu avaḷik kūṟuvar.

avaḷ takuti uṭiyavarkkē kataviṉit tiṟantu viṭuvāḷ. anneṭiya vaḻiyē ceṉṟāl pala katavukaḷ iṭiyiṭum. iṟutiyil ‘oṭṭuk katavu’ oṉṟu tōṉṟum. atāvatu iraṇṭu katavukaḷ oṭṭiya katavu atu ākum. ataṉ uḷḷē ceṉṟāl cittiram pōṉṟa cīrmi uṭiya aḻaki orutti tōṉṟuvāḷ. “aḻivu illāta pēriṉpam etu? eṉpataṟku viṭi tantāl utavuvēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟuvāḷ. takkaviṭi tarāviṭṭālum avarkaḷukku ettakiya tuṉpamum viḷivikkamāṭṭāḷ. neṭu vaḻimaṭṭum kāṭṭuvāḷ; viṭi taruvāri anta mūṉṟu poykikaḷiyum kāṭṭi aṅkē avarkaḷik koṇṭu viṭuvāḷ. avaḷ tirumpiviṭuvāḷ. pañcāṭcara mantirattiyum, aṣṭāṭcara mantirattiyum uccarittuk koṇṭu virumpiya poykiyil muḻukiṉāl virumpiya pēṟukaḷ kiṭikkum. kaṇ kūṭākak kaṇṭa aṉupavam itu; nampic cellalām” eṉṟāṉ.

“ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉik karutāmalum cellalām; tirumāli niṉittu vaḻipaṭṭāl avaṉ karuṭak koṭiyuṭiya tūṇ tōṉṟum. ati vaḻipaṭṭu ittirumāl tiruaruḷ peṟṟu ataṉ piṉ eḷitil maturi aṭiyalām” eṉṟāṉ. “itu pila vaḻi” eṉṟu kūṟi viḷakkiṉāṉ.

iṭip paṭṭa vaḻi

“anneṟi paṭara viruppam illi eṉṟāl iṭiyatu cemmiyāṉa neṟiyākum. cōlikaḷ mikka ūrkaḷ iṭiyiṭṭu varum kāṭukaḷ pala kaṭantāl ariya vaḻi oṉṟu akappaṭum. aṅkē varuttum teyvam oṉṟu kuṟukkiṭṭut taṭukkum. accam tarāta virumpattakka vaṭivattuṭaṉ vantu kāṭci tarum; celvōrit taṭuttu niṟuttum. itu oru iṭiyūṟu, avvaḷavutāṉ; ati mīṟic ceṉṟāl mūṉṟu vaḻikaḷum cantikkum maturip peruvaḻiyi aṭiyalām; piṉ nērē maturi ceṉṟu cēralām” eṉṟu mūṉṟu vaḻikaḷip paṟṟiyum viḷakkamākak kūṟiṉāṉ.

icceytikaḷ aṉittum kēṭṭu avaṟṟiṟku maṟuppu urittār kavunti aṭikaḷ. “pilam pukuntu nalam peṟat tēvi illi; aṅkē poykikaḷil muḻukip puṇṇiyam tēṭat tēvi illi” eṉṟu pukaṉṟār. “puṇṇiya tīrttam muḻuki aintiram eṉṉum nūlip peṟat tēvi illi. arukaṉ aruḷiya meyn nūl tammiṭam uḷḷatu” eṉṟu aṟivittār. “pavakāraṇiyil muḻukip piṟappu aṟiyat tēvi illi. ceṉṟa piṟappiṉ ceytikaḷ ellām intap piṟappik koṇṭu aṟiya muṭiyum” eṉṟār. “iṭṭacitti muḻuki virumpiyatip peṟat tēvi illi; uṇmivaḻi niṉṟu uyirkaḷukku utaviṉāl uyarvu aṭiya muṭiyum” eṉṟu terivittār; av antaṇaṉ aṟivitta aṟivurikaḷi maṟuttuk kūṟi avaṉ kūṟiya iṭi vaḻiyē cellat takkatu eṉṟu tērntu eṭuttār.

maṟiyavaṉukkut tam kōṭpāṭukaḷi aṟivittu avaṉukku viṭi tantu aṉuppi viṭṭuk kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyuṭaṉ aṉṟu anta ūril taṅkiṉār. maṟunāḷ mūvarum puṟappaṭṭuk kāṭṭu vaḻiyē toṭarntu naṭantaṉar.

kavunti aṭikaḷum kaṇṇakiyum ayarntu meyvarunti oru puṟam taṅki ōyvu eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. inta iṭiveḷiyil vaḻiyē oru pakka vaḻiyil tirumpik kuṭikka nīr koṇara oru poykik kariyik kōvalaṉ aṭintāṉ. ataṉ tuṟiyil niṉṟapōtu teyvam oṉṟu avaṉic cantittatu; mātavipāl avaṉ nēyam koṇṭavaṉ. avaṉ virumpum vaṇṇam vayanta māli vaṭivil avaṉ muṉ ceṉṟatu. koṭi naṭukkuṟṟatu pōl avaṉ aṭiyil viḻuntu āṅkuk kaṇṇīr ukuttāḷ. cōkak katiyic cokucākak kūṟiṉāḷ. “mātavi eḻutiya vācakam tavaṟāka uṉṉiṭam yāṉ tirittu uṇarttiviṭṭēṉ. ataṉāltāṉ nī koṭumi ceytu viṭṭāy eṉṟu kūṟik kaṭintu eṉṉi mātavi aṉuppiviṭṭāḷ; avaḷum kaṇiki vāḻkkiyik kiviṭṭu eṉṉiyum veḷiyēṟṟi viṭṭāḷ. ataṉāl maturi cellum vaṇikak kūṭṭattōṭu vaḻi aṟintu uṉṉi vantu aṭintēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi nīlik kaṇṇīr vaṭittāḷ.

avaḷ kūṟṟi avaṉ nampavilli; māṅkāṭṭu maṟiyavaṉ, “mayakkum teyvam vaḻiyil taṭukkum” eṉṟu kūṟiyatu niṉivukku vantatu; uṭaṉē pāy kalippāvi mantiram colli avaḷi accuṟuttiṉāṉ.

avaṉ teyva mantirattukku añci avaḷ uyyum vaḻi nāṭi, “vaṉacāraṇi yāṉ; uṉṉi mayakkam ceytēṉ; icceytiyip puṉamayil cāyalāḷ kaṇṇakikkum, puṇṇiya mutalviyākiya kavunti aṭikaṭkum uriyāmal ēkuka” eṉṟu vēṇṭik koṇṭāḷ. atteyvam avaṉi viṭṭu akaṉṟatu.

avaṉ tāmari iliyil taṇṇīr ēnti ayarvuṟu maṭanti kaṇṇakiyiṉ aruntuyar tīrttāṉ.

antak kaṭum veyyilil pakaṟpoḻutu celvatu aritu eṉṟu karuti aṅkup palvaki maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta aiyi kōṭṭattil oru puṟam aṭintaṉar. vaḻippaṟik koḷḷi ceytu vaḷam koḻittu vāḻum vēṭuvarkaḷ aṅku ak koṟṟavi kōyilukku vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar; palik koṭiyum īntaṉar.





12. koṟṟaviyi vaḻipaṭutal

(vēṭṭuva vari)

kaṭumiyāṉa veyyilil naṭantatāl kaṇṇaki mikka tuṉpatti aṭintāḷ; mūccu vāṅka uyirttāḷ; aiyi kōṭṭattu oru pakkam naṭanta kaḷipput tīra mūvarum iruntaṉar.

akkōyiliṉ maṟupakkattil cāliṉi eṉṉum tēvarāṭṭi orutti teyvamuṟṟu ūr naṭuvē irunta maṉṟattil aṭipeyarttu āṭiṉāḷ “makkaḷ vāḻum nakaraṅkaḷil pacuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ mikku uḷḷaṉa; villēntum vēṭuvar maṉṟukaḷ veṟicciṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷ aṟakkuṭiyiṉar pōl aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉar. koṟṟavikku uṟṟa pali tarāviṭṭāl avarkaḷ veṟṟipeṟa iyalātu; kaḷ peṟṟu makiḻum vāḻkki vēṇṭum eṉṟāl koṟṟavikkup pali iṭṭu mēṟ cellavum” eṉṟu aṟivittāḷ. avarkaḷic ceyalpaṭat tuṇṭiṉāḷ.

vīra marapiṉarākiya avvēṭuvar pali iṭṭut tam taliyi eṇṇuvarēyallāmal nōyil paṭṭu iṟattali avarkaḷ virumpiyatu illi. attakiya vīra marapiṉar tam kuṭip piṟanta kumarip peṇ oruttiyik kōlam ceytu koṟṟavi ākkiṉar. koṟṟavi vēṭam pūṭṭiṉar. avaḷ curuṇṭa kūntali araviṉ kuṭṭi pōlak kāṭci peṟumāṟu kaṭṭi muṭittaṉar; tōṭṭattuḷ mēynta paṉṟiyik koṉṟu ataṉ vaḷinta kompiṉip piṭuṅkip piṟiccantiraṉ eṉac cūṭṭiṉar; pulippallik kōttut tāli eṉa aṇivittaṉar. pulittōli mēkaliyāka uṭuttik kariya niṟattu vil oṉṟiṉi avaḷ kiyil tantu kalimāṉ mītu amarvittaṉar. paṟaviyum, kiḷiyum, kāṭṭuk kōḻiyum, nīla niṟamayilum, pantum, kaḻaṅkum tantu ēttit tutittaṉar. cuṇṇap poṭiyum, kuḷir cantaṉamum, avariyum, tuvariyum, eḷḷuruṇṭiyum, koḻuppuk kalanta cōṟum, pūvum, pukiyum maṟṟum maṇap poruḷkaḷum ēval ceyyum vēṭṭuvap peṇkaḷ ēnti varac ceytaṉar. vaḻippaṟi ceyyuṅkāl koṭṭum paṟiyum, cūṟiyāṭum pōtu ūti olikkum ciṉṉamum, kompum, kuḻalum, perumi mikka maṇiyum aṉittum iyiya olikkac ceytaṉar. akkoṟṟavi vaṭivil kumarip peṇṇi muṉṉāl niṟutti vittu palip pīṭiki muṉ niṉṟu māṉi ūrtiyākak koṇṭa kōyil teyvamākiya koṟṟaviyi vaṇaṅkik kitoḻutu ēttiṉar.

appoḻutu kālaṭi nōvak kaṇavaṉōṭu irunta maṇam mikka kūntali uṭiya kaṇṇakiyi nōkkic cāliṉiyākiya mutumakaḷ toṭarntu teyvam uṟṟup pārāṭṭip pēciṉāḷ. “ivaḷō koṅkac celvi; kuṭamaliyāṭṭi, teṉ tamiḻp pāvi peṟṟa tavac celvi: ulakukkē oḷitarum oppaṟṟa maṇiviḷakku āvāḷ” eṉṟu teyvamuṟṟu urittāḷ.

“immūtāṭṭi pētimi pēciṉāḷ” eṉṟu kūṟiyavaḷāyk kaṇavaṉiṉ mutukupuṟattil otuṅki niṉṟāḷ. arumpiya muralil putumiyit tantāḷ. kumarik kōlattil irunta koṟṟaviyi avvēṭuvarkaḷ pala peyarkaḷic collit tutittaṉar. piṟi cūṭiya perumāṭṭi eṉṟum, neṟṟik kaṇ paṭitta teyvam eṉṟum, pavaḷavāyiṉi uṭiyavaḷ eṉṟum, vaḷiyal aṇinta kiyil cūlam ēntiyavaḷ eṉṟum, ciṅkattiṉ tōli mēkaliyāka aṇintavaḷ eṉṟum, kālil cilampum vīrakkaḻalum aṇintavaḷ eṉṟum, koṟṟam tarum villi ēntiyavaḷ eṉṟum, makiṭācuraṉi veṉṟu avaṉi aṭakki avaṉ muṭimītu aṭivittu āṭiyavaḷ eṉṟum, avvāṭṭattil avaṉ talivēṟu uṭal vēṟu eṉa iruvakik kūṟupaṭṭatu eṉṟum, iṉṉum amari kumari eṉap palvēṟu peyarkaḷil aḻittaṉar. antak kumarippeṇ koṟṟavi kōlattil iruntu avarkaḷukku aruḷ ceytaṉaḷ. iṉi varip pāṭalkaḷ pāṭalām eṉṟu avaḷ āṇiyiṭṭaṉaḷ; ataṉit toṭarntu avarkaḷ pāṭat toṭaṅkiṉar.

aiyi kōyil muṟṟattippaṟṟi mutaṟkaṇ pāṭiṉar. “āṅku nākam, narantam, āccā, cantaṉam, cē, mā immaraṅkaḷ ceṟivu peṟṟaṉa”.

“vēṅki, ilavam, puṉkumaram ivi pūkkaḷic corintu aḻakupaṭuttiṉa”.

“maravam, pātiri, puṉṉi, kuravam, kōṅkam ivi malarntaṉa; ivaṟṟiṉ kompukaḷil vaṇṭukaḷ ārttaṉa; avi yāḻpōl oli ceytaṉa” eṉṟu pāṭiṉar.

aṭuttu avarkaḷ vēṭuvar tam kulattic ciṟap pittup pāṭiṉar.

“koṟṟavi kōlam koṇṭa ippoṟṟoṭi mātu mikka tavam ceytavaḷ; avaḷ piṟanta kulam avaḷāl perumi peṟṟatu.”

aṭuttuk koṟṟaviyic ciṟappittup pāṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. “yāṉit tōliyum, pulittōliyum pōrtti erumit taliyiṉ mēl ēṟi niṉṟavaḷ nī! nī maṟikaḷiyum kaṭanta poruḷāka viḷaṅkukiṟāy. vāṉavarkaḷ uṉṉi vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ñāṉak koḻuntāy nī viḷaṅkukiṉṟāy. attakiya ciṟappi nī paṭittuḷḷāy.”

“kiyil vāḷ ēnti makiṭaṉi aḻittuk kalimāṉ mītu niṟkiṉṟāy. ari, araṉ, ayaṉ immūvar uḷḷattum niṟintavaḷ. nī cōti viḷakkāka avarkaḷ uḷḷattil cuṭar viṭukiṉṟāy”.

“caṅkum cakkaramum uṉ tirukkiyil tāṅki niṟkiṟāy. ciṅka ēṟṟil amarntu irukkiṉṟāy. kaṅkiyi muṭiyil aṇinta civaṉiṉ iṭappākattu uṟikiṉṟāy. vētaṅkaḷ uṉṉi ēttip pukaḻkiṉṟaṉa”.

“maṟṟum koṉṟiyum tuḷavamum cērat toṭutta malarppiṇiyalit tōḷ mēl iṭṭu acurar vāṭa amarar vāḻak kumarik kōlattil marakkāl kūttu āṭikkāṭṭiṉāy”.

“nī marakkālil niṉṟu āṭum pōtu uṉ kāṟcilampu olittatu; cūṭakamum, mēkaliyum māṟi māṟi olittaṉa. avuṇarkaḷ vīḻntaṉar. nī marak kālil āṭumpōtu uṉ kāyām pū pōṉṟa mēṉiyi vāḻtti vāṉōr malar tūṟṟuvar. atu maḻi pōlap poḻiyum”.

veṟṟic ciṟappukaḷ viḷamputal

“accam tarum cīṟūr ataṉil vīraṉ oruvaṉ āniri kavarac celvāṉ eṉṟāl vāḷ ēntiya koṟṟavi ataṉi virumpuvāḷ eṉpatu uṟuti. avaḷ aruḷ ceytāl pakivar ūric cārnta kāṭṭil karikkuruvi taṉ kaṭiya kurali icittuk kāṭṭum; itu uṟuti.”

“kaḷ vili pakarvōr kaṭaṉ koṭukka maṟukkiṉṟaṉar. atip poṟukkāta maṟavaṉ kiyil vil ēntukiṟāṉ. avaṉōṭu paṟavikaḷ avaṉit toṭarkiṉṟaṉa. pakivar pacuniri karuti avaṉ pōkum kālattil avaṉ villiṉ muṉ koṟṟaviyum koṭi eṭuttu avaṉukku veṟṟiyit tara muṉ celvāḷ; itu uṟuti”.

“māmi niṟam uṭiya iḷanaṅkiyē! vēṭṭuvar makaḷē! itaṉik kēṭṭu nī makiḻvāy. ivi niṉ aiyaṉmār mutal nāḷ vēṭṭiyil koṇṭu vantu niṟuttiya pacunirikaḷ; avaṟṟi vēl vaṭittut tanta kollaṉukkum, pōril tuṭikoṭṭiya tuṭiyaṉukkum, yāḻppāṇarukkum āka ivarkaḷukkut tantuḷḷār. avi avarkaḷ muṟṟattil niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivi uṉ aiyaṉmār koṇṭu vanta koḷḷip poruḷkaḷ; veṟṟi virutukaḷ; paricukaḷ; nī perumi koḷvāyāka!”.

“muruntiṉ muki pōṉṟa muṟuval uṉakku aḻaku ceykiṟatu; nī iṅkē pār; uṉ talivarkaḷ karantiyarākiya pakiyāḷikaḷ alaṟak kavarnta pacu nirikaḷ ivi, kaḷviliyāṭṭi, oṟṟu aṟintu vantu kūṟiya kāṉavaṉ, puḷnimittam poruntac coṉṉa kaṇiyaṉ ivarkaḷ muṟṟaṅkaḷil niṟintuḷḷaṉa. itu uṉakkup perumi cērkkum”.

“tāmari pōṉṟa aḻakiya kaṇkaḷi uṭiya iḷanaṅkiyē! niṉ aiyaṉmār ayaluār ceṉṟu ānirikaḷ kavarntu vantuḷḷaṉar. narimutu tāṭiyi uṭiyavarkaḷ; kaṭu moḻi pēciyē paḻakiyavarkaḷ nam vēṭṭuvamakkaḷ; inta eyiṉar eyiṟṟiyar muṉṟilkaḷil intap pacukkaḷ niṟintuḷḷaṉa. iccelvam kaṇṭu nī perumi koḷvāy!”.

palikkoṭi paṭittal

ivvāṟu ivarkaḷ tam veṟṟicciṟappi viḷampip pāṭukiṉṟaṉar. aṭuttu avarkaḷ tammiyē paliyākat tantu koṟṟavikkuc ciṟappuc cērkkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ āṇmiyum, tuṇivum, vīramum itaṉāl pulappaṭṭaṉa; “koṟṟaviyē nī pali ēṟṟuk koḷ” eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉar.

“muṉivarkkum tēvarkkum aruḷ ceyyum teyvamē uṉ iṇiyaṭi toḻukiṉṟōm. aṭal vali eyiṉar niṉṉi vaṇaṅkit tarum palikkaṭaṉ itu, nī tanta veṟṟikku viliyāka avarkaḷ tam miṭaṟu uku kurutiyit tarukiṟārkaḷ; peṟṟuk koḷvāyāka”.

“niṇattil iruntu ukukiṉṟa kuruti ataṉi ēṟṟukkoḷ: itu nī tanta veṟṟikku īṭu ceyvatākum”

“aṭupuli aṉṉa vēṭuvarkaḷ tuṭiyum paṟiyum koṭṭa niṉ aṭikkuc celuttum kaṭaṉ itu palimukattil iṭum kuruti ituvē em kāṇikkiyākum. itaṉi ēṟṟuk koḷvāy”,

“vaḻiiṭi varuvōr mikuti yākaṭṭum; avarkaḷiṭam paṟittut tiraṭṭum koḷḷikaḷ perukuvatu ākaṭṭum. inta vēṭṭuvar paṭikkum maṭippali ēṟṟu aruḷuka”.

“tuṭiyoṭu pakivar ūril aṭivittu eṟivataṟku aruḷ ceyka; ataṟkāka nī palikkaṭaṉ ēṟpāyāka. amutuṇṭu tēvarkaḷ maṭikiṉṟaṉar; nañcuṇṭu nī vāḻkiṟāy. itu viyappāka uḷḷatu; uṉṉip pōṟṟi makiḻkiṟōm; pali ēṟṟu aruḷuka”.

“aruḷ eṉpati neñcakattiliruntu akaṟṟiyavarkaḷ nāṅkaḷ. poruḷ koṇṭu ataṟkāka maṟṟavarkaḷip puṇ ceyvatē em paṇpaṭṭa toḻil, nāṅkaḷ maṟavarkaḷ, nāṅkaḷ iṭum palik kaṭaṉi uṇpāy! nī marutiṭit tavaḻntāy; uṉ māmaṉ vañcakam ceytu cakaṭatti aṉuppiṉāṉ. ataṉi vikaṭam ākki nī veṟṟi koṇṭāy: vīram mikka ceyal atu” eṉṟu pāṭiṉar.

nāṭṭu vāḻttu

iṟutiyil avarkaḷ vēṭṭuva varippāṭal nāṭṭu aracaṉ vāḻttil muṭikiṟatu.

“maṟimutu mutalvaṉ ākiya civaperumāṉ avaṉ taṉ āṇi kēṭṭu akattiyaṉ potiki maliyil taṅkiṉāṉ; appotiki malikkut talivaṉākiya pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ pakivarkaḷ nāṭṭil pōrkkaḷamum, karantip pōrum ciṟappatāka. pakivarkaḷ aḻivu peṟuka! pāṇṭiyaṉ veṭcip pōril veṟṟi peṟuvāṉāka!” eṉṟu avarkaḷ varippāṭal muṭivu peṟukiṟatu.





13. puṟañcēriyil taṅkiya ceyti

(puṟañcēri iṟutta kāti)

payaṇam toṭartal

kumarip peṇṇiṉ koṟṟavi kōlam nīṅkiyatu. āṭimuṭittu aṭaṅkiya piṉṉark kōvalaṉ kavunti aṭikaḷ kālaṭiyil viḻuntu, “ikkoṭiya veyyilil kaṭiya vaḻiyil koṭiyaṉiya kaṇṇaki cellutal iyalātu; paral veṅkāṉattil avaḷ cīṟaṭi paṭiyātu. iravil celvatē takkatu” eṉṟu viḷakkik kūṟiṉāṉ.
